# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## [Unreleased]

- fix(serve): **strip `.git` suffix from `HUGIT_SERVE_GIT_DIR` bare-path slugs — bare dirs like `src.git` are now served as `src`.** `parse_git_dir_member` kept the suffix, so every `GET /src/info/refs` and `POST /src/git-upload-pack` 404'd while `GET /src.git/info/refs` worked. Now strips exactly one trailing `.git` from bare-path basenames before `is_safe_repo_slug` check. Bare `.git` alone → empty → `is_safe` fails (correct), double suffix `repo.git.git` → `repo.git` (single strip), `my.git-tools` preserved. Explicit `slug=path` form unchanged. Test `git_dir_member_bare_path_strips_dot_git_suffix` added. `crates/hugit-serve/src/state.rs`. Gate green (`cargo test -p hugit-serve --lib`).

- fix(cli): **exit-code law + key-order — bug-class internal faults now exit 1, wire `kind` preserved.** `CampaignError` and the intent `PorcelainError` twin gain an `internal: bool` flag. New `internal()` ctor (`kind="internal"`) and `internal_with_kind(kind,...)` ctor (preserves wire `kind` for agents that key on it, e.g. `serialize` in 3 campaign sites). Hand-rolled `PartialEq` ignores the flag (two errors with identical wire fields compare equal). `exit_code()` now branches on `internal` -> `INTERNAL_FAULT_EXIT` (1) for bugs, `PORCELAIN_ERROR_EXIT` (2) for domain. 3 call sites converted: `undo/mod.rs:109` (kind "internal"), `campaign/close.rs:64,165` and `campaign/world.rs:749` (kind "serialize", wire preserved). 2 intent sites: `intent/mod.rs:178` and `intent/canonical_log.rs:276` (bug-class persist). Tests: `internal_fault_preserves_kind_and_equals_domain` (PartialEq ignores flag, wire preserves kind, exit 1). Adjusted two pre-existing tests (`persist_log_io_fault`, `log_fail_leaves_no_orphaned`) to use parent-as-file (the new auto-init defeated the old non-existent-dir failure mode). `crates/hugit-cli/src/{campaign/output.rs, undo/mod.rs, campaign/{close,world}.rs, intent/{error.rs, mod.rs, canonical_log.rs, new.rs}}`. Gate green (`cargo test -p hugit-cli --lib` 238/238 pass).
fix(cli): **auto-create parent dirs in `FileLock::acquire` + `atomic_write` — closes auto-init failure on fresh CWD.** First verb on a fresh dir used to fail `create lock .hugit/log.json.lock: No such file or directory` because `try_create` requires the parent to exist. Best-effort `create_dir_all(parent)` before lock + write, skipped if parent is empty (bare-filename target). The downstream `try_create` / `File::create` will surface a real I/O error if mkdir legitimately failed. Tests: `atomic_write_creates_missing_parent_dirs`, `file_lock_acquire_creates_missing_parent_dirs`, `file_lock_acquire_bare_filename_skips_empty_parent`. `crates/hugit-cli/src/pr/filelock.rs`. Gate green (`cargo test -p hugit-cli --lib filelock` 9/9 pass).
fix(serve): **pkt-line overflow now returns `PktTooLong` instead of `debug_assert` wire corruption — closes BUG-4.** `put_pkt`/`pkt_line`/`strip_want_have_caps` return `Result<_, PktTooLong>`. Bounded callers (constants, validated ref names, fixed reason tokens) use `.expect()`. The single unbounded caller (`strip_want_have_caps` on a client-controlled want/have line) propagates the error to `prepare_upload_pack` which maps to 400 Bad Request instead of emitting a 5-digit-hex corrupt frame. `is_valid_refname` raised to `pub(crate)` for `load_git_dir` to validate ingested repo refs (follow-up). Tests: `put_pkt_size_boundary` (65531 OK / 65532 Err), `build_report_status_roundtrip_bounded`. `crates/hugit-serve/src/{receive_wire.rs, git.rs}`. Gate green (`cargo check --workspace --locked` + `cargo clippy -p hugit-serve -- -D warnings` + `cargo test -p hugit-serve --lib` 862/862 pass).

Best-effort `create_dir_all(parent)` before lock + write, skipped if parent is empty (bare-filename target). - fix(proto): **LCS DP inner-deadline + 8k line cap — closes the 250B-cell BUG-2 DoS wedge.** `lcs_len` now takes `deadline: Option<Instant>` and checks every 64 outer rows, returning `None` on expiry (caller aborts walk with partial diff). `blob_numstat` adds `deadline` param and `MAX_DIFF_BLOB_LINES=8192` cap, counted via `memchr` (no Vec alloc for pathological 1 MiB blobs). Returns `Ok(Some((a, r)))` for real, `Ok(Some((0, 0)))` for cap/binary, `Ok(None)` for deadline — caller maps None to "stop walk". New dep `memchr = "2.8"` (lock already has 2.8.2). Tests: `lcs_len_past_deadline_returns_none`, `lcs_len_no_deadline_completes`, `blob_numstat_over_line_cap_is_honest_zero`, `blob_numstat_under_cap_computes_real_numstat`. `crates/hugit-proto/src/read/pack/mod.rs` + `crates/hugit-proto/Cargo.toml`. Gate green (`cargo check -p hugit-proto --locked` + `cargo clippy -- -D warnings` + `cargo test -p hugit-proto --lib` 78/78 pass).

- fix(serve): **pkt-line overflow now returns `PktTooLong` instead of `debug_assert` wire corruption — closes BUG-4.** `put_pkt`/`pkt_line`/`strip_want_have_caps` return `Result<_, PktTooLong>`. Bounded callers (constants, validated ref names, fixed reason tokens) use `.expect()`. The single unbounded caller (`strip_want_have_caps` on a client-controlled want/have line) propagates the error to `prepare_upload_pack` which collapses `Err` to `None` via `.ok()?` → **404** (the no-oracle discipline for anon upload-pack: don't leak that the body was malformed vs. the repo is absent). 400 mapping would require a different return type — owner-gated scope. `is_valid_refname` raised to `pub(crate)` (no call-site yet — `load_git_dir` integration is a follow-up). Tests: `put_pkt_size_boundary` (65531 OK / 65532 Err), `build_report_status_roundtrip_bounded` (decodes every emitted pkt-line, asserts each ≤ 0xffff), `strip_want_have_caps_oversize_returns_err`, `strip_want_have_caps_normal_want_line_succeeds`. `crates/hugit-serve/src/{receive_wire.rs, git.rs}`. Gate green (`cargo check --workspace --locked` + `cargo clippy -p hugit-serve -- -D warnings` + `cargo test -p hugit-serve --lib` 864/864 pass).
fix(proto): **LCS DP inner-deadline + 8k line cap — closes the 250B-cell BUG-2 DoS wedge.** `lcs_len` now takes `deadline: Option<Instant>` and checks every 64 outer rows, returning `None` on expiry (caller aborts walk with partial diff). `blob_numstat` adds `deadline` param and `MAX_DIFF_BLOB_LINES=8192` cap, counted via `memchr` (no Vec alloc for pathological 1 MiB blobs). Returns `Ok(Some((a, r)))` for real, `Ok(Some((0, 0)))` for cap/binary, `Ok(None)` for deadline — caller maps None to "stop walk". New dep `memchr = "2.8"` (lock already has 2.8.2). Tests: `lcs_len_past_deadline_returns_none`, `lcs_len_no_deadline_completes`, `blob_numstat_over_line_cap_is_honest_zero`, `blob_numstat_under_cap_computes_real_numstat`. `crates/hugit-proto/src/read/pack/mod.rs` + `crates/hugit-proto/Cargo.toml`. Gate green (`cargo check -p hugit-proto --locked` + `cargo clippy -- -D warnings` + `cargo test -p hugit-proto --lib` 78/78 pass).
fix(serve): **centralize slug normalization — `normalize_repo_slug` helper + `validate_name` rejects trailing `.git`.** `resolve_repo_slug` now normalizes first (scoped `a/b.git`→`a/b`, case-sensitive, single strip) so the existence probe `has_repo_seam`/`load_verified` always keys on the canonical bare form. `validate_name` in provision now returns `400 INVALID_REQUEST` for any name ending in `.git`, closing the orphan-tenant path where `POST /v1/repos {name:"foo.git"}` stored `org-a/foo.git` distinct from the engine-resolved `org-a/foo`. Tests `normalize_repo_slug_cases` (11 cases) + extended `invalid_names_rejected_400` (now 12 cases). `crates/hugit-serve/src/{state.rs, writes/verbs/write_provision.rs}`. Gate green (`cargo check -p hugit-serve --locked` + `cargo test -p hugit-serve --lib`).
fix(cli): **`hugit policy edit` now gates `--principal` as Human-only BEFORE the D14 append — a non-human actor can no longer be recorded as the author of a Human-only `policy.change`.** Found live extending QA coverage: `--principal agent:main` was ACCEPTED because `EventLog::append_authorized(PrincipalClass::Human, Endpoint::Policy)` is a **caller-asserted** guard — it trusts the class handed in and never inspects the principal label, so a `user`-supplied `agent:`/`orchestrator:`/`model:`/unrecognized principal walked straight through and was written into the `principal_chain` of a policy-mutating event, breaking the "policy is Human-only" audit-trail honesty. Fix: `do_run` classifies the label with `PrincipalClass::classify` and returns `authz_denied`/exit-2 **with no record appended** unless it is `user:`/`human:` — mirroring `undo`'s chain-classified route (which was already correct). Regression tests cover every non-human prefix (`agent:`/`orchestrator:`/`model:`) + an unrecognized identity, all refused fail-closed with zero append. `crates/hugit-cli/src/policy/edit.rs`. Gate green (fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + hugit-cli lib tests, 234 passed).
- fix(serve): **close two HIGH-severity Art.17 completeness holes on the IRREVERSIBLE erase path — cleartext surviving in the per-tenant registry, and an unreachable post-claim self-heal — both found by a final adversarial re-audit of the merged #317/#319 code.** A bug on this path means an erasure reports "complete" while the subject's cleartext identifier survives forever. **HOLE 1 (unconditional): cleartext `clerk:<org>:<user>` survived in the per-tenant repo registry `_tenants/{account}.json`.** Two coupled defects: (1a) the registry write door (`register_repo_in_tenant`/`unregister_repo_from_tenant`, reached from provision + the reconcile-on-conflict accrual sites) BYPASSED the #319 forward-pseudonymisation — it appended the caller's cleartext `principal_chain` verbatim; and (1b) `shred_and_redact_on_execute` NEVER enumerated `_tenants/`, so after a "completed" erase (`cleartext_shredded:true`, key shredded LAST) the registry cleartext survived and — the pseudonym no longer being derivable — could NEVER be redacted afterward. Fixed at the ROOT: the registry doors now build on the cleartext principal (so the D14 `asserted_class` still sees the real caller) then rewrite JUST the appended tail's stored chain to `subj:<hmac>` via the SAME `repseudonymize_tail` mechanism #319 uses (native re-hash, chain still verifies, no marker); and a new `redact_tenant_registry` pass runs in `shred_and_redact_on_execute` BEFORE the key shred, hash-preservingly redacting any residual cleartext (kill-switch-OFF / legacy records). The fold keys on the `repo` payload, never the principal, so the cap denominator (count/ownership) does not regress. **HOLE 2 (fault-conditional, live — auto-execute is ON): the post-claim shred+redact self-heal (#317) was UNREACHABLE in production.** A transient durable fault (R2 5xx / the runner `ENOTFOUND`) in the post-`erasure.executed` PII step leaves the account reading `executed` with cleartext un-redacted + the key un-shredded (pseudonyms still reversible); both prod callers gate on `read_standing_erasure_request`, which treats `executed` as superseding (`None`) → the operator route 404s and the auto-sweep skips, so the self-heal was dead code (only a direct-call unit test exercised it — a false-green). Fixed by adding a SEPARATE background PII-completion reconciler leg to the EXISTING auto-executor sweep (`auto_execute_one`): a pure predicate `pii_shred_incomplete` (executed present ∧ `pii_shredded` absent) + `should_reconcile_pii` (mutually exclusive with the full-cascade path) detect the stranded state and idempotently re-drive `shred_and_redact_on_execute` to convergence (fail-closed, catch_unwind-isolated + env-gated like the sweep, None-tolerant read-only key path so a converged account no-ops with no key re-mint). The standing-request supersession semantics are UNCHANGED (executed still supersedes the erase cascade). Also corrects stale `provenance_pii.rs` module docs that still described the #317/#319 write-path wiring + durable key store as UNSHIPPED follow-ups. Tests drive the PRODUCTION doors and go RED on revert: an e2e that PROVISIONS through the real door then erases and asserts `_tenants/{account}.json` has no `clerk:`/`<account>:user-1` cleartext + `verify_chain` passes + a fresh registry write stores `subj:`; a legacy-cleartext registry redaction e2e (isolates 1b); and a stranded-executed reconcile e2e driven through the sweep's `auto_execute_one` (converges: cleartext gone, key shredded, `pii_shredded` recorded; no-op on the second pass) + the pure-predicate truth tables. `crates/hugit-serve/src/{state.rs,provenance_pii.rs,provenance_pii_redact.rs,writes/erasure.rs}` + the `art17_erasure_e2e` suite. Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + lib + e2e).
- feat(serve): **complete Art.17 provenance-PII erasure — durable per-subject keys, pseudonymise-from-origin writes, redact-existing on execute, and the executor wired FAIL-CLOSED into the irreversible erase path (#317, ADR-0004; completes the #313 first slice).** The append-only provenance chain still carried the subject's cleartext identifiers (the `account` slug + `clerk:<org>:<user>` in every `principal_chain`), so a "completed" erasure left residual PII forever. This lands the full ADR-0004 model: **(1) durable per-subject key store** — a CSPRNG key per subject persisted create-only (R2 `If-None-Match` / Local `O_EXCL`, no clobber) under `_subject_keys/{subject}.key`; `shred` writes a `SHREDDED` tombstone (atomic temp+rename on Local) that decodes to `None` AND blocks a future create-only mint, so a shredded subject is **non-re-mintable on BOTH backends**. **(2) pseudonymise-from-origin writes** — new provenance records store a keyed-HMAC `subj:<hex>` pseudonym (the chain hashes the pseudonym → `verify_chain` integrity preserved), never the raw identifier. **(3) redact-existing on execute** — `shred_and_redact_on_execute` renders the already-written cleartext unrecoverable via a hash-preserving redaction of the account log + every tombstoned repo log (redaction markers keep `verify_chain` passing), retaining only a lawful Art.5(2) pseudonymous accountability record, THEN shreds the key LAST. **(4) executor wired fail-closed** — the redact+shred runs ONLY on a genuinely completed, `recorded` `erasure.executed` claim (a `partial`/`cancelled` path — including a clean completion superseded by a cancel via #309's `!recorded` guard — NEVER reaches it, so the key stays intact and the subject's non-erased data stays resolvable); a durable fault aborts with `503` BEFORE any success is reported, and the `AlreadyExecuted` retry path RE-DRIVES redact+shred to convergence (self-heals a post-claim durable fault, and redacts the `erasure.executed` record's own cleartext) — so the executor can **never report success while cleartext survives**. Step 1 is None-tolerant + accountability-disambiguated (no `503`-forever on the shred tombstone). Reconciled on rebase with #309's per-leg cancel guard (the `!recorded → Cancelled` early-return gates ahead of the shred). `crates/hugit-serve/src/{provenance_pii.rs,provenance_pii_redact.rs,state.rs,writes/erasure.rs,lib.rs}` + `crates/hugit-refstore/src/{lib.rs,redact_record,tamper/redaction.rs}` + `docs/adr/0004-*` + the `art17_erasure_e2e` suite (incl. the `already_executed_retry_self_heals` fail-closed proof). Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` + `-p hugit-refstore` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test).
- feat(app,serve): **env-var fallback for the GitHub App secrets — makes the DEPLOYED engine (Cloudflare Container, no on-disk secret dir) wireable via wrangler-forwarded env secrets.** `AppAuth` now resolves the App private key from `HUGIT_GITHUB_APP_PRIVATE_KEY` and the app-id from `HUGIT_GITHUB_APP_ID` (env) when the on-disk `private-key.pem`/`app-id` files are absent (the container path); `credentials_present` honours either source; the webhook route resolves the secret from `HUGIT_GITHUB_APP_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (env, precedence) else the on-disk `webhook-secret`. The `installation-id` env fallback already existed. NEVER logged/formatted; fail-closed (absent → CredentialsUnavailable / route DISABLED 404). Live-proven separately: a minted installation token from the real `hugit-dev` App posted a real Check-Run to GitHub (the embrace-GitHub outbound ladder works end-to-end). Owns `crates/hugit-mirror/src/outbound/auth.rs` + `crates/hugit-serve/src/handlers/github_webhook.rs` + this entry.
- fix(serve): **the erasure-execute cancel-race fix, rebuilt HONEST + per-leg — SUPERSEDES the #102 `AbortedSuperseded → 409` model with one coherent contract on the product's most irreversible path (#309).** An independent adversarial audit of #102's guard found it aborted a mid-drive cancel by leaving the repos tombstoned-but-unclaimed and returning a bare `409` — a **dishonest `erased:false` over real, irreversible destruction** and an incoherent account-log-vs-repo-log state, plus the guarded span was the WHOLE cascade (all tombstones + the byte-level GC), not a point. The rebuilt model: (1) **per-leg re-check at every point of no return** — `account_erasure_superseded_by_cancel` re-reads the FRESH account log before EACH `tombstone_repo` AND, inside `drive_cas_gc`, before EACH physical `erase.erase` (each destructive leg is a single-op TOCTOU, not a whole-cascade window); (2) **the honesty invariant (MF1)** — the executor NEVER reports a cancel/`erased:false` once `tombstoned>0 || digests_erased>0`: a withdrawal caught BEFORE any destruction → the new non-destructive `ErasureOutcome::Cancelled` (→ **200**, zero erase, repo intact); a withdrawal that lands AFTER destruction began → the TRUTHFUL terminal outcome — `Executed` (coherent with the erased repo logs, with a `late_cancel_overrun` flag in the account log for audit) if the cascade completed, else `Partial` (→ 202, `outstanding:"halted-by-cancel-mid-cascade"`) with the remaining destruction HALTED; (3) **a `partial` can never clear a standing cancel (MF3)** — `ERASURE_PARTIAL_KIND` no longer resets the superseded predicate, so no later auto-sweep finishes an erasure the subject withdrew. The `AbortedSuperseded` variant + its `409` arm are REMOVED (no dead variant, no dual model); **no consumer receives a 409 from `/v1/account/erase/execute` anymore** — a caller distinguishing "superseded" keys off `state:"cancelled"` (clean) vs `state:"partial"` (`outstanding:"halted-by-cancel-…"`). `append_account_claim` gains `allow_over_cancel` (records the truthful `executed` when destruction happened; still refuses a FALSE `executed` on a clean completion over a cancel). `crates/hugit-serve/src/{writes/erasure.rs,server.rs}` + the MF1/MF2/MF3 tests + 3 `reconcile_old102_*` tests re-proving the old #102 call sites under the unified model. Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test, 839 pass).
- feat(checks): **merge-as-re-execution DISPATCH ORCHESTRATION — the whitepaper marquee (§6.3 regenerative rebase), built hermetically behind the one external fabricd-spawn seam.** The re-execution demand (`agent(ctx_start(I), charter(I)) on workspace(B1)`) was RECORDED by the landing/regen layer but never DISPATCHED (the intentional P2 deferral CLAUDE.md names). New `hugit-checks/src/runner/reexec.rs` closes the hugit-side gap: (1) a recorded `ReExecDemand` (intent + charter + ctx_start ref + target tree `B1` + the four frozen lease params) with `acquire_request()` → the `deny_unknown_fields` `AcquireLeaseRequest` and deterministic content-addressed `def_digest()`/`memo_key()` (whitepaper §6.2 axes, re-dispatch keys identically); (2) `dispatch_reexec()` drives the FULL off-box §13 lease lifecycle (acquire → §13.2 ingest submit with the SCOPED cred → close, always releasing the lease) via the EXISTING `dispatch_attest_offbox` over the pluggable `RunnerTransport` seam; (3) folds in the fail-closed `cost_attestation_verdict` (the #306 CONSUME side) so the outcome carries the cost's integrity, not just its figure; (4) returns a TOTAL honest sum `ReExecOutcome` — `ReExecuted { result, metrics (fabric's finalized signed close figure, full cache-split), cost: CostAttestation, succeeded }` or `Unavailable { reason, detail }`. **Honesty law enforced at the type level:** a dispatch that cannot reach the fabric (acquire refused → `FabricUnreachable`, box unwired → `BoxNotWired`, no §13 ingest cred → `IngestNotWired`, lease not held, exec refused) is `Unavailable` — NEVER a fabricated success; a re-execution that RAN but failed its acceptance gate (`exit_code != 0`) is `ReExecuted` with `succeeded=false` (the caller MUST NOT land it); the lease is always closed (no leaked capacity); the PAT never leaks into an `Unavailable` detail. The ONE remaining external gate to go live is the corelink-runners fabricd agent-spawn (`POST /v1/leases/{id}/exec` bringing a real agent loop up) — everything up to it is built + hermetically tested against a fake transport (10 new tests: full lifecycle, valid-sig→Attested / absent-sig→EmissionOff / no-config→NoPubkey cost folding, failed re-exec, acquire-refused, no-ingest fail-closed with lease still closed, total error→reason mapping, PAT non-leak, deterministic demand shaping). `crates/hugit-checks/src/runner/{mod.rs,reexec.rs}`. Gate green (`-p hugit-checks` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--lib`, 88 pass).
- feat(mirror): **advance the "embrace, don't assault" GitHub compat ladder three rungs — hermetically, behind the owner's App-install seam.** Builds the hugit-side logic so the ONLY thing missing to go live is the owner's GitHub App install + a test repo (the runner fabric + a live install stay external gates). (1) **Landing rides ON GitHub (B1③/E3).** `ChecksClient` (`hugit-app`) now POSTs a real GitHub **Check-Run** AND a **commit status** back to the repo through a fake-testable `ChecksTransport` seam (real `UreqChecksTransport`, Bearer = the now-real `mint_installation_token`); the `StatusEmitter::emit_live` glue mints an App token then posts the verdict, returning an honest `LiveStatusOutcome::{Posted, NotConfigured, Failed}` — absent creds / uninstalled App → **NotConfigured (PARTIAL, never a fabricated post)**, non-2xx/decode/transport fault → **Failed (fail-closed)**. `conclusion_to_status_state` projects one verdict onto both surfaces. (2) **Stateful uninstall revoke (B1⑤).** A new durable `RevocationLedger` (`hugit-app`, on-disk tombstones under `~/.hugit/state/revoked`, path-traversal-safe ids, restart-surviving) is written by `WebhookProcessor::handle_uninstall` (`durably_revoked` in `RevokeOutcome`) and ENFORCED fail-closed at two gates: `AppAuth::mint_installation_token` refuses a revoked installation **before** any disk/sign/network (new `AppAuthError::InstallationRevoked`, checked ahead of the token cache), and `PersistenceAdapter::new_local_with_revocations` re-seeds its halted set from the ledger so a restarted process re-halts a revoked installation's events. (3) **Bounded one-way mirror (the safe rung).** `BidirSync::reflect_bounded` + `sync::reflect` reflect forge branch tips → GitHub **idempotently** (re-run emits nothing), **bounded** (≤ N pushes/pass, remainder Deferred), and **conflict-detecting/fail-closed**: a GitHub-side tip that is neither our last-emitted nor the target tip is a divergence that **HALTS the pass and never clobbers** — surfaced for the engine's forge-authoritative arbitration (which preserves the GitHub tip as a recoverable incident). *A broken bridge kills trust; a divergence halts, never clobbers.* **External gate (unchanged):** the owner's live GitHub App install (private key + app-id + installation-id on disk, or `HUGIT_GITHUB_APP_SECRET_DIR`) + a `HUGIT_GH_TEST_REPO`; the live GitHub-side change *detect* (webhook/poll) stays the documented P2 seam (`sync::detect`), and the batched **inbound** reflect leg is STOP-reported as the next WP. All hermetic (fake transports, zero network): a check-run/status constructed+posted against a fake GitHub transport; absent creds → PARTIAL; a revoked installation refuses a subsequent mint+event (durably, across a simulated restart); the bounded mirror reflects idempotently + HALTS on a simulated divergence. `crates/hugit-app/src/{checks.rs,revocation.rs,webhook.rs,persistence.rs,lib.rs}` + `crates/hugit-mirror/src/{outbound/auth.rs,status/emitter.rs,status/mod.rs,sync/{engine.rs,reflect.rs,mod.rs}}` (+ `ureq` on `hugit-app`). Gate green (`-p hugit-mirror -p hugit-app` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--lib`, 95 pass).

- fix(serve): **three MED-severity engine defects — the refs-refresh monotonic guard (W2), the erasure-execute cancel-race TOCTOU (#102), and the boot tenant-registry reconcile (#76).** (1) **W2 refs-refresh monotonic guard** — the WP-B5 background refresher's unconditional `LiveRefs::replace` could transiently REVERT a push's own hot-swap: an unversioned R2 GET issued before a concurrent push's conditional PUT but arriving AFTER the push's `set_ref` clobbered the just-applied new tip with the stale pre-push snapshot for up to one interval (~2 s), re-advertising the OLD tip after the client already got `ok`. `LiveRefs`'s interior is now `{ refs, generation: u64 }` behind the same `RwLock`; `set_ref`/`remove_ref` bump `generation` under the write lock, and the refresher captures `generation()` at read-START then installs via the new `replace_if_unchanged(refs, expected_generation)` — a stale in-flight read can never overwrite a locally-newer hot-swap (R2 ETags are opaque/unordered + `RefsManifest` has no generation field, so a per-repo monotonic local counter is the correct primitive). Absent/fault/malformed fail-safe (keep-current-map) unchanged. (2) **#102 erasure cancel-race** — `execute_account_erasure_inner` re-planned + re-checked `account_already_executed` but did NOT re-read `read_standing_erasure_request` between the caller's grace/standing check and the terminal claim, so — now that G12a (#296) ships a LIVE `erasure.cancelled` producer — a cancel landing in that window would NOT be honored and the cascade could claim `executed` for an erasure the user just cancelled. Two guards close it. **PRIMARY (point of no return, before ANY physical erase):** immediately before the tombstone loop the drive re-loads the account log and, if `classify_erase_cancel` reports `AlreadyCancelled`, ABORTS with ZERO physical erase (`AbortedSuperseded { repos_tombstoned: 0 }`) — the cancel is genuinely HONORED, the repo is never tombstoned (not merely un-claimed). Using `classify_erase_cancel` (not a bare `None` read) means a legitimate no-staged-request direct/operator drive and a matured standing request BOTH proceed — only an already-cancelled governing state aborts. **SECONDARY (after the loop, before the terminal claim):** re-reads the governing request vs the drive-start capture; if it changed (a cancel landing DURING the loop — too late to prevent the erase) it still blocks a false `executed`/`partial` claim. Server maps `AbortedSuperseded` → 409. Adds aborts only on a genuine change; never weakens an existing gate. (3) **#76 boot tenant-registry reconcile** — the `_tenants/{org}.json` registry is a best-effort SECOND durable write after genesis, so a transient R2 fault on the register PUT → a persistent undercount that lets different-slug creates accumulate past `MAX_REPOS_PER_TENANT`; legacy git-ingested repos (hugit/githugr) were also never registered. A new off-loop boot reconcile (`spawn_boot_reconcile_tenant_registry`, detached + `catch_unwind` + bounded, CAS-mode gated, never blocks boot — mirrors `boot_build_pat_index`/the refs-refresh loop) enumerates `list_repo_slugs()` and, for each durable repo whose genesis verifies, ensures it is present in its owner tenant's registry via the idempotent, genesis-authoritative `reconcile_tenant_repo` (heals the undercount, back-fills the legacy repos, keeps the cap honest — never double-counts, never registers a foreign-owned repo). `crates/hugit-serve/src/{state.rs,server.rs,writes/erasure.rs}` + 6 new hermetic tests (incl. the primary before-erase cancel abort). Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--lib`, 827 pass).

Two guards close it. **PRIMARY (point of no return, before ANY physical erase):** immediately before the tombstone loop the drive re-loads the account log and, if `classify_erase_cancel` reports `AlreadyCancelled`, ABORTS with ZERO physical erase — the cancel is genuinely HONORED, the repo is never tombstoned. **SECONDARY (after the loop, before the terminal claim):** re-reads the governing request; if it changed it blocks a false claim. **NOTE — this #102 model (incl. the `AbortedSuperseded { repos_tombstoned }` → 409 outcome) is SUPERSEDED within this same unreleased cycle by the honest per-leg reconciliation (#309, see the bullet above): the abort is now the non-destructive `Cancelled`/200 when nothing was destroyed, else the truthful `Executed`/`Partial` (never `erased:false` over real destruction), and no 409 is emitted from this route.** (3) **#76 boot tenant-registry reconcile** — the `_tenants/{org}.json` registry is a best-effort SECOND durable write after genesis, so a transient R2 fault on the register PUT → a persistent undercount that lets different-slug creates accumulate past `MAX_REPOS_PER_TENANT`; legacy git-ingested repos (hugit/githugr) were also never registered. A new off-loop boot reconcile (`spawn_boot_reconcile_tenant_registry`, detached + `catch_unwind` + bounded, CAS-mode gated, never blocks boot — mirrors `boot_build_pat_index`/the refs-refresh loop) enumerates `list_repo_slugs()` and, for each durable repo whose genesis verifies, ensures it is present in its owner tenant's registry via the idempotent, genesis-authoritative `reconcile_tenant_repo` (heals the undercount, back-fills the legacy repos, keeps the cap honest — never double-counts, never registers a foreign-owned repo). `crates/hugit-serve/src/{state.rs,server.rs,writes/erasure.rs}` + 6 new hermetic tests (incl. the primary before-erase cancel abort). Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--lib`, 827 pass).
- feat(serve): **Art.17 leg 2 — forward write-path pseudonymisation: cleartext PII no longer ENTERS the provenance chain going forward (ADR-0004).** Every live write verb's record-append now stores the subject's `subj:<hmac>` pseudonym in the `principal_chain` instead of the cleartext `clerk:{org}:{user}`. The coordination is centralised in the two write doors (`with_write`/`with_account_write`) plus the provision-genesis + token append paths via a `WriteIdentity` seam: the verb body still runs on the LIVE cleartext chain (its D14 class + `owner_tenant` derivation are UNCHANGED — authz keys on the projected `owner_tenant` + the live request principal, never the stored chain), then the door FORWARD-pseudonymises the freshly-appended tail and re-hashes it — sound because `this_hash` excludes the class/endpoint, so the rewritten records verify NATIVELY (fresh hashes, no redaction marker), zero verb-signature churn, zero `hugit-refstore` change. Every tail record is re-chained UNCONDITIONALLY, so a mixed-principal multi-record verb (a `clerk:` record followed by an `orchestrator:` passthrough) keeps a correct `prev_hash` linkage. **FULL-PRINCIPAL granularity, per-ACCOUNT key:** the pseudonym HMACs the WHOLE `clerk:{org}:{user}` (not the bare org) under the org's durable key, so two distinct users in one org get DISTINCT pseudonyms — the principal-keyed idempotency ledger keeps per-user granularity (two users sharing an Idempotency-Key on the same verb+resource do NOT collide) and Art.5(2) per-user accountability, while an account erase still shreds exactly ONE key and renders ALL its per-user pseudonyms unrecoverable. **The idempotency hazard #317 flagged is eliminated, not worked around:** the `idem.recorded` ledger stores the pseudonym AND `idem_lookup` DUAL-MATCHES cleartext-OR-pseudonym, with the pseudonym match arm reconstructed READ-ONLY and FLAG-INDEPENDENTLY (never gated on the kill-switch), so a re-submitted identical verb dedups to exactly ONE execution across BOTH kill-switch flip directions AND the pseudonymisation-migration boundary → NO double-execute on the live forge. Wired ON by default with an ops kill-switch (`HUGIT_SERVE_PROV_PSEUDONYM=0|false|off`) that governs only whether NEW writes are pseudonymised, never whether the lookup considers the pseudonym. The load-bearing PAYLOAD identity fields the live readers key on — `repo.meta.owner_tenant` (authz) + `pat.created.user` (PAT auth) — are deliberately retained cleartext forward (pseudonymising them needs the identity-reader refactor leg 3 scoped out) and are rendered unrecoverable at erase by leg 4's redaction (idempotent over already-pseudonymous forward records — no double-handling). Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/{state.rs (`WriteIdentity` impl + `pseudonymize_write_chain` + kill-switch), provenance_pii_redact.rs (full-principal HMAC + read-only `readonly_principal_pseudonym`), writes/mod.rs (`WriteIdentity` trait, `repseudonymize_tail`, flag-independent dual-match `idem_lookup`, door threading), writes/verbs/{write_provision,write_token}.rs, server.rs (call sites)}`; proven by unit tests (door-level dedup + no-cleartext-chain + kill-switch-flip no-double-execute both directions + mixed-principal re-chain) and end-to-end `art17_erasure_e2e` tests (real key store: forward write dedups + owner authz survives; two distinct users in one org do NOT collide in the idempotency ledger; a completed erase renders the forward pseudonym unrecoverable). Closes ADR-0004 leg 2 (all five legs now DONE).

- feat(serve): **content-addressed home-render cache — kills the ~0.5–1 s per-request `/home` CAS tree-walk on the single-threaded lazy-CAS engine.** `GET /v1/repos/{repo}/home` listed the root tree + resolved the README on EVERY request (each `src.get` a synchronous R2 fetch blocking the whole accept loop), and it did NOT warm — `build_home` re-walked per request even for an unchanged tree. New `HomeRenderCache` (`crates/hugit-serve/src/home_cache.rs`) caches the two CAS-EXPENSIVE outputs (the raw tree listing + README bytes) keyed by the **content-addressed root TREE OID**: a HIT is a pure in-memory op (**ZERO CAS tree-walk** — proven by a `PanicSource` test), a MISS runs the existing `WALK_BUDGET`-bounded walk ONCE then populates. Because the key IS the tree oid, a push produces a new tree → a new key → an automatic MISS (a changed tree can NEVER be a stale HIT — no rebuild hook needed for correctness; a budget-TRUNCATED walk is served-but-never-cached so a later live read re-walks). The FAST log-derived parts of the VM (branch/counts/last-commit/contributors) are rebuilt FRESH per request from the verified log — NEVER cached. Stored UNSCRUBBED; every emitted path/README line is `scrub`-ed at the READ boundary (mirrors `search_index`/`blob_history_index` — never a redaction bypass). Byte-bounded (64 MiB of content bytes) with FIFO eviction (mirrors `cas::BoundedObjectCache`). Held on `AppState.home_cache`, threaded via `RepoGit` to `build_home`; an off-loop boot pre-warm (`bootstrap_home_caches`) + a post-push/-delete pre-warm (`spawn_home_cache_prewarm`, `catch_unwind`-isolated, single-flight, never blocks) so the first read after a boot/push is a HIT. Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/{home_cache.rs (new),handlers/home.rs,state.rs,server.rs,git.rs}` + 9 new hermetic tests (HIT-is-zero-CAS, MISS-then-HIT, new-tree-oid-is-a-MISS-no-stale-serve, byte-cap eviction, read-boundary redaction over raw storage, log-parts-stay-fresh). Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--lib`).
- fix(serve): **two convergence-re-audit HIGH findings on the go-live wave's irreversible/DoS surface (both dormant, fixed at root before merge).** (1) A `_`-prefixed repo name (`_foo`) passed `validate_name` and persisted to `<tenant>/_foo.json`, but `LogSource::list_repo_slugs`'s R2 arm deliberately skips `_`-prefixed keys as reserved — so after a reboot (runtime overlay lost) the repo was INVISIBLE to the GDPR erasure enumeration + the exclusive-digest partition, holing the "durable-but-unloaded repo can never be missed" guarantee on the IRREVERSIBLE erase path (worst case: a surviving user's `_`-repo dropped from the surviving set → its shared CAS object mis-classified exclusive → physically erased). Fixed by RESERVING the `_` prefix — `validate_name` now rejects a leading underscore (a non-leading `_`, e.g. `repo_1`, stays valid), so no `_`-named repo can exist and the enumeration filter is correct by construction. (2) The GitHub App webhook's PRE-AUTH reject path (`webhook.rejected` audit on a bad/absent signature) did an inline `sync_all()` fsync per request on the single-threaded accept loop, so an unauthenticated flood could force a per-request blocking fsync. Audit appends (`webhook.rejected`/`installation.revoked`) are now NON-fsync (best-effort, OS-buffered — a crash may lose the last few monitoring records, acceptable); only the accept-path `enqueue_envelope` (reached AFTER a valid HMAC + the rate limit) keeps the durability barrier so the 202 is never a false ack. Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/{writes/verbs/write_provision.rs,handlers/github_webhook.rs}` + 2 new name-validation cases + this entry. Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--lib`).
- feat(checks): **the CONSUME side of the cost-killer off-box attestation (WP-1 propagation + WP-2 verdict) — buildable-now, hermetic; live-green gated externally on the runners fabric flipping `intent_metrics_sig` emission.** (1) `DispatchOutcome` now carries the verify binding OFF the close: `lease_id` (previously surviving only as `runner_ref="offbox:{id}"`), the fabric's `intent_metrics_sig: Option<String>`, and `fabric_key_id: Option<String>`. `dispatch_attest_offbox`/`attest_and_collect` read them from the `CloseResponse` alongside the finalized `metrics`; the B-path (`dispatch_check`) is unaffected (sig/key `None`). `CloseResponse.fabric_key_id` was promoted from a tolerated-and-ignored extra to a modelled additive field (`#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if)]` — still liberal, no `deny_unknown_fields`, wire byte-identical when absent). (2) New `cost_attest` module: `cost_attestation_verdict(config, &outcome) -> CostAttestation::{Attested, Unattested(reason)}` calls `verify_intent_metrics_sig` FAIL-CLOSED — `Attested` ONLY when a PRESENT sig verifies; absent sig ⇒ `Unattested(EmissionOff)`; present-but-invalid ⇒ `Unattested(SigInvalid)` returned DISTINCTLY (a hard fabric/MITM signal); no/blank pubkey ⇒ `Unattested(NoPubkey)`. Renders/emits NOTHING (a `✓ cas:`/`​/insights` render is owner-gated on a real non-zero cost). (3) The pubkey/tenant seam is `FabricAttestConfig{fabric_pubkey_b64, tenant}` (env `HUGIT_FABRIC_PUBKEY_B64`/`HUGIT_FABRIC_TENANT`, `from_env` fail-closed to `None` on absent/blank — no hardcoded key, no live fetch). Added a lossless reverse `From<IntentMetrics> for RunnerJobMetrics` so the verdict reconstructs the exact bytes the fabric signed. HONESTY LAW held: an `Attested` is impossible without a verified sig. 8 new hermetic tests (propagation of sig/key/lease; the four verdict cases + wrong-tenant anti-replay + malformed-sig fail-closed). Owns `crates/hugit-checks/src/{cost_attest.rs (new),lib.rs,runner/dispatch.rs,runner/metrics.rs,runner/lease_client.rs}` + this entry. Gate green (`-p hugit-checks` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--lib`; wire_conformance integration test also green).
- feat(serve): **a REAL precomputed in-memory code-search index for `/search` (new `crates/hugit-serve/src/search_index.rs`) — removes the per-request CAS grep, the latency-DoS that wedged prod.** `GET /v1/repos/{repo}/search?q=` used to serve its `code` results by walking the git tree and `src.get`-ing every blob AT REQUEST TIME (`handlers::search::search_code`). On the single-threaded lazy-CAS engine each `src.get` on a cold cache is a SYNCHRONOUS R2 fetch that blocks the WHOLE accept loop (incl. `/readyz`); a single query fanning out thousands of sequential fetches wedged prod once (the 2026-06-27 code-search wedge — bounding by result-count doesn't help, the cost is per-OBJECT-FETCH). Replaced with a precomputed `SearchStore` (`slug → (head, indexed files)`) built OFF the accept loop, MIRRORING `blob_history_index`: `bootstrap_search_indexes` at boot + `spawn_search_index_build` after each push/delete, each a DETACHED, `catch_unwind`-isolated, single-flight-guarded thread that walks the repo's live-HEAD tree ONCE and records each file's path + a bounded content prefix. The `/search` request path (`build_search`, now taking a `CodeIndexQuery` instead of a git source) consults the in-memory index ONLY — an O(matches) scan, wall-clock-bounded — and on a MISS (no index yet / stale HEAD / a repo that isn't indexed) returns honest-empty; it NEVER falls back to a live CAS scan. **Bounded everywhere:** build = 30s wall-clock + 5 000 files + 64 KiB/file + 32 MiB/repo + a 256 MiB GLOBAL byte cap (the index can't become an OOM vector); query = 500ms wall-clock + 200 total + 10/file. **Visibility-gated at BUILD time:** only a PUBLIC (anonymous-readable) repo is indexed — reusing `authorize_read(&[], meta)`, the SAME predicate the read gate uses, so a private/erased repo has NO index entry and can never leak content via `/search` (defence-in-depth atop the per-request read gate the route already runs). **Redaction preserved:** content is stored UNSCRUBBED and every emitted match line + path is `scrub`bed at the read boundary (same discipline as `blob_history_index`/`handlers::search`). The old interim `search_code` git-tree grep + its bounds constants are removed. 14 new hermetic tests (build records paths+content bounded; query returns real matches; a private/unindexed repo returns nothing; an unbuilt index → honest-empty with NO source fetch — a `PanicSource` proves it; stale-HEAD refused; per-file + global byte caps hold; the zero-budget query guard; redaction at the read boundary; the public-only visibility predicate; single-flight build slot; the global-byte-counter accounting; `build_search` end-to-end through a seeded index). Owns the new module + touches `state.rs` (the `search_index` field + both constructors), `server.rs` (bootstrap wire + the `RepoGit` bundle + the `/search` dispatch arm), `git.rs` (the push/delete rebuild hooks), `lib.rs` (module decl), `handlers/search.rs` (`build_search` signature + the removed grep) + this entry. Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--lib`, 740 pass). Live rendering waits on a redeploy (built ≠ live).
- feat(serve): **`/commits` + `/commit/{sha}` render REAL git substance for a git-pushed-but-never-landed repo (the flagship `githugr` was EMPTY).** Root cause: `git-ingest`/`git push` write the git object closure to CAS but ZERO event-log records, so `project_machine(log)` yields 0 rows → `/commits` empty, `/commit/{sha}` 404. Fix (honest, option-(b)-as-(c)): read the machine altitude from the ACTUAL git DAG in CAS and UNION it with real log rows — **NEVER synthesize `intent.landed`/log records** (the forbidden #113 fabrication trap). New shared WP0 helper `commit_meta_from_cas` (`handlers/commit_meta.rs`) — a thin `gix_object::CommitRef` decode (author/message/commit-time/parents), missing/undecodable → `None`. `build_commits` now takes `git_source`/`head_commit`/`refs`: a bounded first-parent CAS walk (`WALK_BUDGET` wall-clock + `COMMITS_CAP` count, mirroring `blob_history`/`build_home`) emits a `CommitRowVm` per commit with REAL author/subject/commit-time/sha and `intent_id: None`; oid-keyed union where a real `project_machine` row WINS over a git-only row for the same oid; the branch selector unions replay(log) refs with the live git `refs`. `build_commit_detail` takes `head_commit`: on no log-row match it resolves the sha (full oid direct, or short prefix via the bounded head-walk) DIRECTLY from CAS → REAL title/author/age + the existing diff-vs-first-parent numstat, `external: true`, honest-empty checks/cost/provenance. `/landing` is unchanged (the landing board never shows git commits — a regression test pins the honest-empty board) and `/intent/{sha}` stays an honest 404 for a non-landed commit. `git = None` → the original log-only path, unchanged (no regression). Every rendered field is REAL git data or honestly absent; NOTHING is written to the append-only log. 10 new hermetic tests (WP0 decode/missing/non-commit; 3-commit chain on an empty log → 3 real rows; intent-wins union; git-none unchanged; CAS-fallback via short prefix + full oid; honest 404 without seam/head; landing empty board). Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/handlers/{commit_meta.rs,commits.rs,commit_detail.rs,landing.rs}` + `server.rs` dispatch arms + the two parity tests + this entry. Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--lib`: 741 passed).
- feat(serve): **GDPR1 erasure AUTO-EXECUTOR — an env-gated, fail-closed background sweep that auto-completes matured Art.17 erasure requests off the accept loop (goes to clw re-audit BEFORE the env is flipped on).** The go-live audit found two coupled holes: (1) a staged `erasure.requested` (202, 7d grace) NEVER auto-completes — no cron/scheduler exists, so a real user's request sits indefinitely, breaking Art.17's bounded-window obligation; and (2) the manual operator execute cascade DoSes the single-threaded accept loop (an O(all-platform-repos) partition scan + per-digest blocking erase, run INLINE on the request). This adds `AppState::spawn_erasure_auto_executor_loop` — a DETACHED thread structured EXACTLY like `spawn_refs_refresh_loop` — that fixes BOTH: it runs the WHOLE cascade on the sweep thread (never blocking the accept loop, the DoS fix) and periodically drives every matured governing request to completion (the Art.17 fix). Per sweep it enumerates account slugs (`LogSource::list_account_slugs`, the same durable enumeration the PAT boot-index uses), reads each account's standing request (`read_standing_erasure_request`), and for a MATURED + governing + DSR-legitimate request drives the STANDARD `execute_account_erasure_composed` — the identical cascade the manual route uses, so every gate (the DSR-id legitimacy, the exact-superset subject−surviving partition, the durable `executed`/`partial` terminal claim, idempotency) is enforced UNCHANGED; it adds NO custom erase path and does NOT touch the manual operator route (an in-flight Track-A verify uses that). **FAIL-CLOSED + env-gated (DEFAULT OFF):** the loop spawns ONLY when `HUGIT_ERASURE_AUTO_EXECUTE=1|true` AND the engine is in CAS mode AND `state.erase_config` is configured (pure `should_spawn_auto_executor` guard, mirroring `receive_pack_enabled`/`allow_multi_instance`) — a routine deploy NEVER auto-fires an irreversible erasure. Slow cadence (`ERASURE_AUTO_EXECUTE_INTERVAL_MS` = 5 min, a reconciler not a latency path), per-account ISOLATED (a load/execute fault OR a panic is logged + SKIPPED via `catch_unwind`, never halts the sweep or crashes the thread), idempotent/resumable (an already-`executed` account is a no-op, a `partial` retries next sweep), and bounded (`ERASURE_AUTO_EXECUTE_MAX_PER_SWEEP` = 32 drives/pass, remainder next sweep). Acts as the OPERATOR principal (`crate::server::dev_principal` = `orchestrator:hugit`, same as the manual route); the SUBJECT is read from the standing record, never invented. 9 new hermetic tests: the PURE maturity predicate `is_matured_governing_request` (matured→true, before-grace→false, superseded→false, saturating-no-wrap) + the PURE selection `should_auto_execute` (matured+DSR→drive; not-matured/superseded-or-absent/no-DSR-id→skip) + the PURE spawn guard truth table (spawns only on enabled∧CAS∧erase-seam; every single-gate-off → fail-closed no-spawn). Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/{state.rs,server.rs,writes/erasure.rs}` + this entry. Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--lib`, 740 pass). **Env-gated + fail-closed — goes to clw re-audit before the env is flipped on at go-live.**
- feat(serve): **durable per-tenant repo REGISTRY (WP-1, the multi-tenant keystone) — fixes three audit-confirmed defects at the source.** A new reserved `_tenants/{org}.json` keyspace (mirroring the `_accounts/` GDPR1 store: the same `LogSource` fetch/persist + canonical-JSON + chain-verified `EventLog` create-or-append discipline) holds the AUTHORITATIVE per-tenant owned-repo set/count, in a new `crate::tenant_registry` module (`tenant.repo_registered`/`tenant.repo_unregistered` records, folded to a set; register/unregister are idempotent). **(A) reboot cap-bypass / durable undercount** — `count_owned_repos` no longer counts the in-memory boot∪runtime union (lost on reboot → a tenant could re-provision past `MAX_REPOS_PER_TENANT`); it reads the DURABLE registry count, stable across engine lifetimes. **(B) O(all-platform-repos) accept-loop scan** — the old count called `load_verified` (a chain-verified R2 fetch) per candidate ON the single-threaded accept loop on every `POST /v1/repos`; the registry count is ONE small per-tenant log fetch → O(1) w.r.t. the platform. It is also the substrate the later erase-execute O(M) surviving-repo scan (#98-G1) reuses. **Atomicity/self-heal:** the genesis create stays the FIRST, authoritative durable write; the registry register is the SECOND (a repo is never counted before it durably exists). A crash BETWEEN them leaves an un-countable repo that SELF-HEALS — a retry of the same slug reconciles it (`reconcile_tenant_repo`, genesis is the source of truth: re-register a genesis-without-registry-entry, never a foreign/absent repo) before the 409; register is best-effort (the repo is durably created + readable, the count heals on the next touch). **Erase decrement:** the GDPR1 erase-tombstone loop unregisters each tombstoned repo (the cap is a HOLD-count, erasable-down; best-effort so a decrement fault leaves the SAFE over-count direction, never fails the irreversible cascade). **Fail-closed:** an indeterminate registry read (transport fault / tamper-parse failure) → `count_owned_repos` returns `None` → the provision refuses (a read fault can never slip past the cap); an ABSENT registry is a genuine empty owned-set (`Some(0)`), never a block on a first create. The `org` key reuses the `is_safe_account_slug` `[a-z0-9-]≤64` identity (ONE identity: ownership ≡ erasability ≡ registry key; a malformed org → 404, no `_tenants/../evil.json` traversal). Changes are localized (a new module + the `count_owned_repos` repoint + two seam methods on `LogSource`/`AppState` + the two call-sites in `write_provision`/`erasure`). 11 new hermetic tests (fold set-semantics/idempotency/corrupt-record-skip; CAS roundtrip + scoped-not-a-repo + traversal-refused; count reads the registry; **count survives a reboot and holds the cap**; fail-closed on a corrupt registry; erase decrement is a hold-count; **reconcile re-registers a mid-write-crash genesis, idempotent, never a foreign/absent repo**) + the existing count/cap tests migrated to the durable-registry source. Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/{tenant_registry.rs,state.rs,lib.rs,writes/verbs/write_provision.rs,writes/erasure.rs}` + this entry. Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--lib`).
- feat(app,mirror,serve): **GitHub App LIVE-TRANSPORT wiring — webhook ingress (WP-1) + real installation-token minting (WP-2)**, wiring live transport onto the already-frozen, hermetically-tested mirror/App logic ("embrace, don't assault"). **WP-1 (webhook ingress):** a new PRE-AUTH route `POST /v1/github/webhook` (`crates/hugit-serve/src/handlers/github_webhook.rs` + one localized arm in `server.rs::route_write`, mirroring the `/v1/token` no-Bearer arm). GitHub authenticates via the HMAC `X-Hub-Signature-256` (verified through the frozen `hugit_app::WebhookProcessor`), NOT a Bearer. On a valid signature the door DURABLY enqueues the `SignedEventEnvelope` (fsync'd append-only NDJSON journal under `HUGIT_GITHUB_WEBHOOK_QUEUE_DIR`/`~/.hugit/webhook-queue`; the CoreLink-CAS target is the prod seam) and returns **202 IMMEDIATELY** — it NEVER runs ingest/arbitration/mirror/git-push inline (that would starve the single-threaded accept loop, THE DoS surface). Bad/absent signature → **401** + a durable `webhook.rejected` audit record (chained seq+prev_hash); `installation.deleted` → `WebhookProcessor::handle_uninstall` + `PersistenceAdapter::halt_installation` + a durable `installation.revoked` record, still fast-ack. Absent webhook secret (`webhook-secret` file in the App secret dir) → the route is DISABLED (404), never a forgeable half-config. A failed durable enqueue → 503 (GitHub retries), never a false ack. Rides the loop's existing bounded-body-read + shed/503 admission model. **WP-2 (installation-token minting):** `AppAuth::mint_installation_token` (`crates/hugit-mirror/src/outbound/auth.rs`) no longer returns a fake `local-proof` handle — it signs a short-lived RS256 App JWT (`jsonwebtoken`, from the on-disk PEM + app-id) and exchanges it at `POST /app/installations/{id}/access_tokens` over a single bounded-timeout `ureq` transport behind a `TokenTransport` trait (unit-testable with a fake, ZERO network — mirrors `lease_client.rs`). The token is cached with a refresh-before-expiry deadline (parsed from GitHub's `expires_at`, per-installation keyed) and FAILS CLOSED on any error (missing material → `CredentialsUnavailable`; 404 → `RepoNotCovered`; signing/transport/non-2xx/decode → the new `JwtSigning`/`ExchangeFailed` variants), NEVER fabricating a usable token; the minted token redacts in `Debug` and never appears in a log/error. The Verified live path still needs the owner's real App install (out of scope). The App JWT signing is factored apart from the exchange/cache core so the latter is proven with ZERO key material — no private key is committed (respecting the repo's `*.pem` secret-hygiene ignore). 13 new hermetic tests (WP-1: valid HMAC→202+enqueued, bad/absent sig→401+rejected-audit, `installation.deleted`→halt+revoked, empty-secret fail-closed, audit-chain links; WP-2: fake-transport models a real token + forwards the JWT, error/404/empty→fail-closed PARTIAL never a token, TTL cache + expiry-refresh, per-installation cache isolation, RFC-3339 parse, missing-installation-id→unavailable, `sign_app_jwt` rejects a bad key fail-closed). Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/handlers/github_webhook.rs` + the `server.rs` arm + `crates/hugit-mirror/src/outbound/{auth.rs,mod.rs}` + this entry. Gate green (`-p hugit-app -p hugit-mirror -p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + `test --lib --locked`).

- fix(serve): **two auth-hardening fixes on the engine-token path (`token.rs` + a `state.rs` boot guard).** (F1) The step-up `fresh_auth` bit was bound at mint (`fva ≤ 5`) but the token's TTL (≤300s) was NOT trimmed, so a token minted at `fva=5` lived another ~5 min → true staleness AT USE reached ~10 min, double the documented `< FRESH_AUTH_MAX_FVA_MINUTES` invariant. Now the freshness predicate is STRICT (`0 <= fva < 5`; a boundary mint has no usable fresh window) AND a fresh token's TTL is trimmed to the freshness remainder `(FRESH_AUTH_MAX_FVA_MINUTES - fva) * 60` s (`min` with the upstream remaining), so total elapsed-since-reauth stays `< 5 min` for the whole token life; `expires_in` reflects the trimmed TTL. A non-fresh token keeps its full upstream-bounded TTL (unchanged). (T1) `TokenStore::from_env` silently falls back to a per-boot RANDOM signing key when `HUGIT_ENGINE_TOKEN_KEY` is unset/blank — so a secret-provisioning slip on a `max_instances>1` deploy would silently reproduce the pre-#128 ~50% 401 cross-instance failure. Added a fail-closed boot guard (`engine_token_key_multi_instance_guard`, mirroring the sibling `pat_auth_multi_instance_guard`) wired into `AppState::from_env`: multi-instance permitted + key absent/blank → refuse to boot with a message naming the failure and the fix. 5 new hermetic tests (F1: fva=0 → full-window TTL, fva=4 → 60s, fva=5 → not-fresh strict, non-fresh keeps full TTL; T1: the pure guard's three cases). Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/{token.rs,state.rs}` + this entry. Gate green (`-p hugit-serve` fmt + clippy `--all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test).
- fix(serve): **harden the #96 lazy-load-on-miss against four audit-confirmed defects (L1/L2/L3 + W1) — merged-not-yet-deployed, the redeploy was HELD on this.** (L1, CRITICAL — accept-loop stall) `repo_state_or_load` ran the load INLINE on the single-threaded accept loop, reachable PRE-AUTH (git `info/refs`), and its CAS gets went through `with_throttle_retry!` (~12s of `thread::sleep` on 429/503). The lazy path now runs a BOUNDED `LoadMode::LazyLoad`: a new `LAZY_LOAD_FETCH_TIMEOUT` (3s) tight-timeout agent for BOTH R2 (`R2Config::get_object_bounded`) and CAS (`UreqCasTransport::get_fast`, no retry macro), so a single lazy-load stalls the loop for at most ~a few seconds, never ~12s+; BOOT keeps the retrying behavior (it is off the request path). (L2, HIGH — varied-slug 404 storm) the per-slug negative cache can't stop a stream of DISTINCT nonexistent slugs on the unauth git wire, each forcing a fresh R2 round-trip; added a GLOBAL fixed-window budget (`repo_lazy_load_budget`, a single bounded counter — NOT a per-slug map, so it can't itself leak) allowing at most `MAX_LAZY_LOADS_PER_WINDOW` (20) attempts per 1s to touch R2, over-budget → `None` FAST without touching R2. (L3, MED — transient false-404) `build_cas_repo_state`/`load_manifests_from_cas` now return a TYPED `RepoLoadError` distinguishing **Absent** (`refs.json` GET `Ok(None)` — authoritative non-existence) from **Transient** (5xx/timeout/throttle/decode/partial-seam); only Absent is negative-cached, a Transient miss returns `None` WITHOUT caching (a real repo no longer false-404s for the 30s cooldown), mirroring the transient-vs-authoritative discipline `count_owned_repos`/`owned_repo_logs` apply. (W1, HIGH — provisioned-but-never-pushed cross-instance 404) `provision()` writes only the genesis event-log + an in-memory empty seam on the creating instance (no `refs.json`/oid-index), so a first push/clone on another instance lazy-loaded → refs.json Absent → 404; now an Absent result whose DURABLE GENESIS LOG exists (checked via the existing `load_verified` predicate) builds an EMPTY CAS seam (`build_empty_cas_repo_state` + `insert_runtime_repo`), so create-then-immediate-push works on ANY instance; no genesis log → a true 404 (negative-cached). L4 (repos_runtime unbounded `&'static` leak) is OUT OF SCOPE (tracked separately — needs an Arc/LRU refactor); the L2 budget already bounds how fast it can grow. 12 new hermetic tests (fast-fail vs retrying dispatch + throttle→Transient-fast; typed Absent vs Transient incl. partial-seam + R2-fault; the global budget caps/resets; the decision policy: over-budget-no-R2, Absent+no-genesis→negative-cache, Absent+genesis→empty-seam/W1, Transient→no-cache). Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/{state.rs,cas.rs}` + this entry. Gate green (fmt + clippy `-p hugit-serve --all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `-p hugit-serve --lib`). Deserves an adversarial pass on the accept-loop surface before it rides prod.
- fix(serve): **lazy-load a provisioned repo from R2 on a resolution miss — closes the `max_instances≥2` new-repo-404 + reboot-loss gap (#96).** A repo created via `POST /v1/repos` lands ONLY in the creating instance's in-memory `repos_runtime` overlay (a leaked `&'static RepoState`); boot loads only the static `HUGIT_SERVE_CAS_REPO` comma-list, and `repo_state` 404s anything in neither set — with NO lazy-load. So on the now-live 2-instance engine, a freshly-created repo 404s on git (`info/refs`/upload-pack/receive-pack) + web reads from the instance that did NOT create it (and is lost on ANY reboot, since it's not in the comma-list) — the consistency lag the githugr TL hit live (clw independently root-caused it to the same per-instance-overlay/no-R2-lazy-load cause + recommended this fix). Now `AppState::repo_state_or_load` reconstructs the repo from the DURABLE R2 manifests on a miss (via the boot construction, extracted into the shared `build_cas_repo_state`) + registers it, so ANY instance serves ANY durably-provisioned repo on first request — no reboot, no comma-list edit. Wired at the 5 git-wire entry points + the blob-read + PR-create resolutions. **Accept-loop-SAFE (the load-latency-DoS class this engine has scars from):** CAS-mode only (Local/git-dir keeps the fixed set), store-safe-slug only (never an R2 probe for a traversal string), and a **negative cache** (`repo_load_misses`, 30s cooldown) so a 404-probe storm can't re-hit `load_manifests_from_cas` on every request — a miss is recorded and short-circuited. The single-threaded accept loop means no same-instance load/register race; a hit is identical to the old `repo_state` (no R2). Reuses the SAME loader boot uses (no new R2 LIST / durable index). 2 new hermetic tests (Local-mode passthrough — lazy-load is CAS-gated + never touches the cache; the negative-cache mark/read-back per-slug). Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/{state.rs,git.rs,server.rs}` + this entry. Gate green (fmt + clippy `--workspace --all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test). NON-blocking for the go-live (provisioning is low-volume) + deserves an adversarial pass on the accept-loop surface before it rides prod.
- feat(checks): **pin the shared `intent_metrics_sig` conformance vector — the off-box attested-cost binding is now proven wire-equivalent byte-for-byte (the go-signal for lighting `✓ cas:` cost).** The runners TL committed `conformance/intent_metrics_sig.json` (corelink-runners #320); this mirrors it **byte-identical** into hugit (SHA-256 `0acccaee…`, added to `conformance/manifest.sha256` + the x4 `VECTORS` set) and adds THE fabric-match tripwire test (`attest_v2::intent_metrics_sig_matches_conformance_vector_byte_exact`): hugit's `intent_metrics_preimage` reproduces the fabric's `preimage_hex` byte-exact AND `verify_intent_metrics_sig` accepts the fabric's real signature, plus a +1 µUSD cost-tamper is rejected (proves the sig binds cost). With the twin golden green in corelink-runners on the same committed bytes, the off-box cost binding is proven — no 5th wire-drift. (Dev key, same discipline as `result_binding_v2.json`: the drift risk is the pre-image bytes, key-independent; the prod pubkey stays in `attestation_key_set.json` + `/v1/attestation/key`.) **Remaining to light live `✓ cas:` cost (NOT this PR):** the owner's cost-non-zero decision (holds — honest-zero until a real provider-`/usage` source) → the runners TL flips `FABRIC_EMIT_INTENT_METRICS_SIG` on → hugit captures the sig on the close. Owns `conformance/intent_metrics_sig.json` (new) + `conformance/manifest.sha256` + `crates/hugit-invariants/x4/tests/acceptance_x4_wire.rs` + `crates/hugit-checks/src/attest_v2.rs` + this entry. Gate green (full-workspace x4 validator + fmt + clippy `--workspace --all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test).
- fix(serve): **a malformed `fva_minutes` from the exchange degrades to NOT-fresh instead of failing the whole token mint (Track-B parse hardening).** The just-landed `fresh_auth`-from-`fva` wiring typed `ExchangeOk.fva_minutes` as `Option<u32>` — so a NEGATIVE emit (exactly the bug class the server-TL hit: mishandling Clerk's `fva[1] = -1` no-2FA sentinel) would fail the entire `ExchangeOk` serde decode → the whole `/v1/token` mint `503`s, not just freshness. Too brittle for the critical auth-mint path: a bad OPTIONAL freshness signal must never break authentication itself. Now the field is parsed TOLERANTLY as a raw `serde_json::Value` (`ExchangeOk::fva_minutes_i64` extracts a plain `i64` via `Value::as_i64`, or `None` for a float/string/object/absent), and the step-up predicate is a closed range `(0..=FRESH_AUTH_MAX_FVA_MINUTES).contains(&m)` — so a negative/out-of-range/wrong-type value degrades to NOT fresh (step-up refused) while the token still mints. `FRESH_AUTH_MAX_FVA_MINUTES` is now `i64`. 1 new test (a matrix of `-1`/`-9999`/`"garbage"`/`1.5`/`{}` each ⇒ `200` mint + `!fresh_auth`); the happy-path fresh/stale tests unchanged. Prompted by the server-TL's live `fva[1]=-1` reject diagnostic — hugit's own parse is now bulletproof against that class regardless of the upstream fix. Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/token.rs` + this entry. Gate green (fmt + clippy `--workspace --all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test).
- feat(serve): **step-up `fresh_auth` is now derived from the Clerk `fva` — a real user's browser reauth clears the erasure/policy step-up (GDPR1 Track B, the real go-live gate).** The Track-A verify surfaced that `handle_token_exchange` hard-coded `fresh_auth: false` (comment: "auth_time is not a Clerk session claim") — so NO real Clerk user could ever clear the `["policy","erasure"]` step-up gate, i.e. no real user could stage an account erase (403). The githugr TL found Clerk emits the freshness signal natively as **`fva`** (factor-verification age; `fva[0]` = minutes since the first factor was last verified, `0` right after a reauth), and the corelink-server TL froze it as a pass-through field **`fva_minutes`** on the `/v1/session/exchange` response (server PR #670). This wires the hugit side: `ExchangeOk`/`ExchangeIdentity` gain `fva_minutes: Option<u32>` (serde `default`; the response is the same superset hugit already parses — `token_plaintext`/`pat_id` are still ignored), and `handle_token_exchange` sets `fresh_auth = id.fva_minutes.is_some_and(|m| m <= FRESH_AUTH_MAX_FVA_MINUTES)` (`= 5` min, matching the ≤300s engine-token TTL, so a stale/hijacked session can't originate an irreversible erase without a recent reauth). **FAIL-CLOSED:** an absent `fva_minutes` (a non-reauthed session, OR the exchange not yet emitting the field) ⇒ NOT fresh ⇒ the exact pre-Track-B 403 behaviour — so this deploys safely BEFORE/independently of the server worker deploy (no window where anyone is erroneously fresh). The derived bit is baked into the #278 HMAC-signed engine token, so it can't be tampered post-mint; and it's a VERIFIED value (CoreLink parsed it from the JWT hugit already trusts it to verify — no new trust surface). 2 new tests (fva-fresh ⇒ `fresh_auth` token; fva-stale `> window` ⇒ not fresh) + the "never fresh" exchange test updated to the absent-fva fail-closed semantics. **Not go-live by itself:** this is the last code gate; the `solicitado→apagado` copy flip still waits on the server worker deploy + githugr's fva-fresh `202` re-verify + clw's Track-B re-audit. Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/token.rs` + this entry. Gate green (fmt + clippy `--workspace --all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test).
- feat(checks): **`intent_metrics_sig` verifier — an off-box `✓ cas:` can now attest the COST, not just the tenant (the cost-integrity gap the runners TL and I closed 2026-07-08).** An off-box (A-path) close carries a `result_binding_sig_v2` over an EMPTY `CheckResult` (`check_result: null` ⇒ 20 constant zero bytes), so it binds only the TENANT (via the attestation chain) and the `cost_usd_micros` rides back in `CloseResponse.metrics` UNSIGNED — a real per-PR-attested-cost hole (a third party couldn't prove the cost wasn't altered). The runners TL built the fabric-side fix (`intent_metrics_sig`: an ed25519 signature over the finalized `IntentMetrics` bound to `lease_id`+tenant, anti-replay) and FROZE the byte-for-byte pre-image; this is hugit's verifier + wire adoption. New `hugit_refstore::intent_metrics_preimage` (the single-sourced byte-exact pre-image, mirroring `result_binding_preimage_v2`: `LP(lease_id)‖LP(tenant)‖u64_be×5 tokens‖u64_be wall/active/tool_calls‖u32_be(breakdown.len)‖[LP(tool)‖u64_be(count)]‖u64_be(model_turns)‖u64_be(cost_usd_micros)`, all big-endian, tool-order bound) + `hugit_checks::attest_v2::verify_intent_metrics_sig` (decode key/sig, rebuild the pre-image from the response's `metrics`+`lease_id`+tenant, `verify_strict` — fail-closed on any malformed input, never a panic). `CloseResponse` gains `intent_metrics_sig: Option<String>` (`#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if)]`) — **ABSENT on the wire** unless the fabric sets `FABRIC_EMIT_INTENT_METRICS_SIG` (default OFF), so today's byte-identical close is unaffected and hugit can adopt the field before the fabric emits it. `None` ⇒ cost stays fabric-recorded-but-unattested and `✓ cas:` MUST NOT claim cost integrity (the honest scoping: off-box sig = tenant identity; cost integrity ONLY via this field). 3 new tests: the sig verifies + rejects EVERY tamper (wrong lease_id/tenant, +1 micro-USD cost, reordered tool_breakdown, wrong/malformed key+sig); the pre-image framing is byte-locked by an independent re-derivation; the `CloseResponse` field is wire-invisible when absent + parsed when present + never serializes `null`. **Follow-up (tracked, gates the live `✓ cas:` flip, NOT this PR):** pin `conformance/intent_metrics_sig.json` (a shared vector with the fabric's REAL signature) byte-identical both repos to prove fabric-match — requested from the runners TL — and only then, with the owner's cost-non-zero decision, flip `FABRIC_EMIT_INTENT_METRICS_SIG` + render. Owns `crates/hugit-refstore/src/log/mod.rs` + `crates/hugit-checks/src/{attest_v2.rs,runner/lease_client.rs}` + this entry. Gate green (fmt + clippy `--workspace --all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test).
- feat(serve): **GDPR1 Track-A verify-only stage hatch — proves the irreversible erasure cascade + the physical `410-Gone` leg LIVE without waiting on the real reauth step-up (clw-approved 2026-07-08, 5 hard conditions).** The go-live verify surfaced the real blocker: `handle_token_exchange` hard-codes `fresh_auth: false` (no `auth_time` on the exchange), and the only `fresh_auth: true` sites are `#[cfg(test)]` — so a real Clerk user can NEVER clear the `["policy","erasure"]` step-up gate, i.e. **no real user can even STAGE an account erase** (403). Shipping the compliance copy on a synthetic bypass would be a built≠delivered overclaim, so real go-live moves to Track B (wire real reauth step-up — a cross-repo effort). This entry is Track A ONLY: an env-gated, named-subject, verify-only hatch that lets the designated dev/operator principal stage `erasure.requested` for EXACTLY the one throwaway test subject, so the operator-execute cascade + the real CoreLink CAS `410-Gone` physical-erase leg can be proven against the armed engine NOW (de-risking the highest-risk irreversible code). **FAIL-CLOSED at every arm** (pure, env-free `erasure_verify_stage_subject_pure`, mirroring `grace_secs_with_floor`): absent `HUGIT_ERASURE_VERIFY_STAGE_SUBJECT` (or an empty/non-slug value, or a caller that is not `orchestrator:hugit`, or a missing/mismatched `X-Verify-Stage-Subject` header) it returns `None` and the production path — INCLUDING a real user's 403 step-up — is entirely unchanged. When active it acts AS the named subject by synthesizing `clerk:{subject}:verify-stage` and flowing through the UNMODIFIED production write path (so the verb's `confirm==derived-subject` guard + as-the-user append still hold) — **blast radius = that one env-named account**, never an arbitrary/real subject. **NOT a go-live signal** (a green Track-A verify de-risks the cascade; it does not authorize the `solicitado→apagado` copy flip, which is gated on Track B); the env is set for the verify window then REMOVED (same discipline as the grace-floor override). 4 hermetic tests (the accepting case; fail-closed on every missing/mismatched arm incl. non-slug env; NEVER fires for a real Clerk user or anon even with env+header; the synthesized principal stages for the NAMED subject + carries the DSR id, not the caller). Owns `crates/hugit-serve/src/server.rs` + this entry. Gate green (fmt + clippy `--workspace --all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test). Rides a PR for clw's cold re-audit before any live enablement.
- feat(checks): **pin hugit's own moat-gate `CheckDef` — the verified `toolchain_ref` digest + the exact CI gate command (#68 closed).** With the check-host (B-path) toolchain snapshotted to CAS and the runners TL's hydrate round-trip GREEN (`4e3da22e…` verified complete + self-consistent in tenant `d863fafb`, 168 files / 668,404,519 bytes, all gate tools at `/toolchain/bin`, 2026-07-07), the ratified producer-side contract (`toolchain_ref` = the clw snapshot root digest, no alias — the memo axis equals the hydrated content) is now frozen in code. New `hugit_checks::gate`: `HUGIT_CI_TOOLCHAIN_REF` (the 64-hex digest), `HUGIT_CI_GATE_COMMAND` (`PATH=/toolchain/bin:$PATH` prepend — the check-host does not auto-set PATH — then fmt → clippy `-D warnings` → test → cargo-deny → cargo-audit, mirroring `.github/workflows/ci.yml` step-for-step so a moat run and a CI run are the identical gate), and `hugit_gate_check_def()` (the pinned `CheckDef` with the `def_digest` computed over the canonical body + a workspace-scoped `glob_set`). So at the owner's rota-A flip a check acquire carrying this `toolchain_digest` hydrates exactly this tree and runs exactly this gate — zero further hugit work at flip time (live memoized-check dispatch through the moat stays the P2 that waits on rota-A being on). 3 tests (the ref is a 64-hex content-address not an alias; the command prepends the PATH + runs every CI gate leg; the def_digest is deterministic + non-empty). Owns `crates/hugit-checks/src/{gate.rs (new),lib.rs}` + this entry. Gate green (fmt + clippy `--workspace --all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test).
- fix(checks): **the §13.2 `submit_envelope` now validates the server-supplied `ingest_path` before sending the scoped credential — closes a confused-deputy credential-exfiltration (adversarial-audit HIGH).** `submit_envelope` built the ingest URL as `format!("{}{}", base, ingest_path)` with the `ingest_path` taken RAW from the fabric's acquire response and NO validation — and the docstring falsely claimed "the credential can only ever be sent to the configured host." A buggy or compromised fabric returning `ingest_path = "@evil.com/x"` (→ `https://runner.example@evil.com/x`, authority `evil.com`), `".evil.com/x"`, or `":9999/x"` would send `Authorization: Bearer <scoped §13 ingest credential>` to an attacker host. Now `validate_ingest_path` (mirroring the existing `validate_lease_id` request-target guard) requires a single-leading-slash absolute path (`/…`, not `//`), ≤512 chars, no whitespace/control/backslash — which TERMINATES the URL authority at the base host, so no `ingest_path` can move the request off it — and fails closed with the new `RunnerError::InvalidIngestPath` BEFORE the URL is built or the bearer attached (the credential never reaches the wire; it never appears in the error). The tenant PAT was already never sent here (only the scoped cred), so blast radius was bounded — but a real scoped credential exfil, triggerable by a fabric bug not just malice, is closed at root. 2 new tests (the validator accepts the frozen `/v1/leases/{id}/envelope/ingest` shape + rejects every authority-injection vector; `submit_envelope` refuses a hostile path with zero transport calls + no secret in the error). Also from the same audit: (a) **`project_intent_metrics` now caps the per-count expansion** at 10 000 events/kind — the counts are a hugit-side `measured` input, but a corrupt one near `u64::MAX` would OOM; the projection is only the submitted trajectory (the attested figure is the fabric's finalized close), so clamping is safe; (b) the `dispatch_attest_offbox` `cost_usd_micros` docstring is **reconciled with the shipped caller** — it flatly said "provider-billed, NEVER derived," but the land caller passes the owner-ratified WP-COST-3 Option A figure (`Σ` this PR's REAL `ctx.usage` tokens × the stamped published rate); the doc now names both truthful sources and keeps "forbidden = fabricated/estimated/misattributed," so a future off-box-loop author isn't misled; (c) a **FORWARD-GAP note** on the land caller: when the off-box loop is wired, `land` must refuse to settle on a failed off-box verdict (inert today, `exit_code` hardcoded `0`). Owns `crates/hugit-checks/src/runner/{lease_client.rs,dispatch.rs}` + `crates/hugit-cli/src/pr/dispatch.rs` + this entry. Gate green (fmt + clippy `--workspace --all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test).
- fix(checks+cli): **an off-box run that FAILED can no longer be mis-attested as a success — `dispatch_attest_offbox` takes an explicit `exit_code` (#94 follow-up).** After the no-box-poll fix, the off-box A-path synthesized its `CheckResult` with a HARDCODED `exit: 0`, so `dispatch_attest_offbox` always closed the lease `succeeded` — a latent honesty hole: once the P2 off-box agent loop is wired, a run that FAILED would still attest a green close. Now `dispatch_attest_offbox` (and its `attest_and_collect`) take the run's own `exit_code` (`0` = completed successfully), carry it onto the synthesized `CheckResult.exit`, and the close status derives from it — so `exit_code != 0` closes `failed`, never mis-attested. The current caller (`hugit pr land --dispatch` → `dispatch_pr_metrics`) passes `0` with a documented rationale: that path is only reached AFTER the PR resolved + the land gate passed, so the attested run is a completed success; a live off-box loop that could fail will thread its real exit. 1 new hermetic test (a failed off-box run closes `failed`, the verdict rides `CheckResult.exit`) + the 4 A-path hermetic tests + the live orchestration smoke updated for the new arg. Owns `crates/hugit-checks/src/runner/dispatch.rs` + `crates/hugit-checks/tests/runner_apath_live_smoke.rs` + `crates/hugit-cli/src/pr/dispatch.rs` + this entry. Gate green (fmt + clippy `--workspace --all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test). (The other #94 item — capturing the live `result_binding_sig_v2`/`fabric_key_id` to light `✓ cas:` — stays deferred pending the runners TL confirming what an OFF-BOX v2 signature binds, since the off-box `check_result` is `null`.)
- fix(checks+cli): **the off-box cost-killer A-path no longer polls a box result envelope — the flagship `hugit pr land --dispatch` path now completes against a REAL fabric (was fake-transport-only).** Driving hugit's real `LeaseClient` + `dispatch_attest_offbox` against the LIVE Cloudflare fabricd (2026-07-07) surfaced a 4th same-class wire bug (after the acquire-response #204 / close-body #205 / acquire-request #214 drifts): `attest_and_collect` submitted the §13 trajectory then **`poll_meta`-decoded `GET .../envelope/meta` as a box-exec `RunnerResultEnvelope{exit,…}`**. An OFF-BOX lease runs no box, so live the fabric returns the §13.2 event echo (`{"meta":[]}`) and `close.check_result` is `null` → the decode failed `missing field exit`. It only ever passed against the fake transport (the never-run-live blind spot). **Fix:** on the off-box A-path, DON'T poll a box result — synthesize the off-box `CheckResult` from the stamped axes (`exit 0` = completed run; off-box `runner_ref`); the caller (`hugit pr land --dispatch`) uses ONLY `DispatchOutcome.metrics`, which `dispatch_attest_offbox` already supersedes with the fabric's FINALIZED close metrics (the signed source of truth).
