a path-overlap `false` is a sound conservative serialize); names the overlapping keys. **(2) `land-status`** shells the REAL `hugit queue show --log <path>` porcelain (binary resolved via the `hugit_bin` arg › `$HUGIT_BIN` › `hugit` on PATH) and parses its stdout JSON, passing the porcelain's null-verdict honesty (`null` = "no verdict recorded yet", never a faked pass/fail) through verbatim; a `{"error":{…}}` envelope surfaces as a tool error; `log` is REQUIRED (no silent `.hugit/log.json` fallback in an arbitrary cwd). **(3) `cost-attest`** reads `GET /v1/repos/{repo}/insights` with a real Bearer (auth-gated), projecting the integer micro-USD fields + `spend_proof` CAS refs; **STRUCTURALLY refuses any hand-stamp override** (any `cost`/`cost_usd_micros`/`override`/`stamp`/`spend_proof` argument is rejected — the #113 demo-stamp reversal mechanized), classifies each campaign attested-or-not (non-zero cost **AND** a `spend_proof`), and reports **per-PR raw cost as honest-null** until the runner fabric supplies a provider-billed figure (no derived/misattributed COGS). **(4) `liveness-probe`** hits `/readyz` (unauthenticated) + an OPTIONAL bounded authed probe of a LIGHT endpoint with a **git User-Agent** (Cloudflare bot-protection returns `403 error 1010` to unknown UAs), disambiguating **401-auth-gate** (NOT route-absence) / **403-bot** / **404 denied-or-missing** (no-oracle, with vs without a token) / **2xx-visible**; and it **REFUSES heavy reads** (`search`/`diff`/`compare`/`history`/`blob`/`insights`/`tree`/`edit`) as a pure input-validation guard that fires BEFORE any network call — a count-bounded (not wall-clock-bounded) read wedges the single-threaded lazy-CAS engine's whole accept loop (including `/readyz`) for minutes. Bounded connect/read timeouts; bodies trimmed to a snippet. Lean deps (only `hugit-queue` + `serde`/`serde_json` + the already-workspace-pinned `ureq`; no new crate/version in the lock). 36 hermetic tests (stdio round-trip, per-tool arg-validation + honesty guards, the fake-`hugit` shell e2e, the no-oracle 404 disambiguation, the heavy-path refusal as a pure guard). Wired into the workspace (`crates/*` glob) + the gate (fmt + clippy `--workspace --all-targets --locked -D warnings` + test `--workspace --locked` all green).

- feat(serve): **REAL per-file diff numstat on the compare / PR-detail / commit-detail reads** (was a universal empty `DiffVm` stub). The three handlers now compute an actual `git diff --numstat`-shaped changed-file list (path + added/removed line counts) from the per-repo git source (the CoreLink-CAS-backed `ObjectSource`) + the live refs, via the `hugit_proto` tree-diff. **`compare/{base}/{head}`** resolves each ref-ish (an exact ref key, then `refs/heads/{name}`/`refs/tags/{name}`, then a raw 40-hex oid) against the live ref snapshot and diffs the two commits' root trees (base→head). **`prs/{n}`** diffs each bundled intent's landed commit against its first parent (per-intent `IntentSummaryVm.diff` + the PR-level `diff`/`file_count`/`added`/`removed` rollup from the tip intent). **`commit/{sha}`** diffs the matched commit against its first parent. **Wall-clock bounded:** every numstat rides `hugit_proto::DIFF_BUDGET` (the `tree_diff` walk stops at the deadline with its partial result — the single-thread read-latency-DoS guard); each read runs exactly one diff. **Honest-empty fallback** (never a fabrication): no git source, an unresolvable ref/oid, a root commit (no first parent), or a fail-closed tree-walk → an empty `DiffVm`. **Security:** every file path is SCRUBBED at the read boundary (a secret-shaped repo path must not echo into the view-model) — covered by a per-handler secret-MATRIX test. New shared `diff` helpers (`diff_compare` / `diff_commits` / `diff_against_first_parent`) reuse the existing `diff_vm`; tests prove a real 2-commit numstat, the raw-oid fallback, the root-commit + no-git honest-empty paths, and the scrub. (The deleted false stub comment in `compare.rs` is removed.)
- feat(serve): **authenticated clone-back over the git wire — a private repo is cloneable by its OWNING tenant** (go-live cross-tenant READ gate). Before, the upload-pack routes passed an EMPTY principal to `authorize_read` (`git.rs` `authorize_read(&[], …)`), so a PRIVATE repo was uncloneable by ANYONE — including its owner. Now both upload-pack routes (the `GET …/info/refs?service=git-upload-pack` advertise AND the `POST …/git-upload-pack` body) extract an OPTIONAL `Authorization: Bearer <token>`, validate it via the IDENTICAL Tier-1 path the receive-pack write side uses (`TokenStore::lookup` — constant-time SHA-256 + expiry sweep, the same store `two_tier_auth` consults), and derive the real `clerk:{org}:{user}` principal fed to `authorize_read` (new `clone_principal` helper). Result: **anonymous** (no/invalid/expired Bearer) is unchanged — public clones, private → 404 (no oracle); the **owning tenant** clones its PRIVATE repo; a **foreign tenant** (valid Bearer, wrong tenant) → 404 (cross-tenant isolation — never learns the repo exists). **Fail-CLOSED:** a malformed/expired/garbage token degrades to anonymous (public-only), NEVER to "authenticated as someone"; a store fault is `Invalid` → anonymous. **No operator bypass on the read wire:** `clone_principal` DELIBERATELY does NOT consult the Tier-2 dev-token fallback, so the `orchestrator:*` god-principal (`authorize_read` → sees-all) can never be acquired over the clone wire — the clone principal is ONLY a validated tenant or anonymous (go-live: a real user gets no god-token). **Both routes gate identically** (the POST runs the same derivation + `authorize_read` as the advertise — no split-route bypass where a pack is served for a repo the advertise hid). 6 go-live-grade tests (real-`git` e2e where applicable): owner-tenant authed clone of a private repo succeeds; anon-on-private → 404; foreign-tenant → 404 (advertise + POST); garbage Bearer falls back to anonymous (public still clones, private → 404); POST gates identically to the advertise. The existing public-clone + push e2e stay green.
- fix(serve): **epoch-0 `updated_ago` honesty** (githugr NEEDS #4) — the repo-home `about.updated_ago` humanized a 0/absent `recorded_at` into a fabricated "há 56 anos" (a 1970 epoch) that the consumer rendered as a real last-update. Now emits EMPTY for a 0 timestamp (`.filter(|r| r.recorded_at > 0)`), never an invented date. Regression tests for epoch-0→empty + real-timestamp→humanizes.

- fix(serve): **scrub git ref/branch names at the read boundary** (read-path audit 2026-06-30). Branch names became ATTACKER-CONTROLLABLE when `git push` went live, but `compare` (HIGH — `branches`/`generated_branches` echoed unscrubbed while `base`/`head` were scrubbed), `home` (`branch`/`branches`), and the `branches` default-branch fallback echoed them RAW → a secret-shaped branch name could leak to a viewer. Now all ref short-names pass `scrub`. Also scrubbed LOW payload echoes (`intent_detail` agent_type/run_id/parent_run_id/cas_ref/context_cas/compact_transcript_ref; `security` erasure request_id). `secret_shaped_branch_name_is_scrubbed` regression test added. (Honesty axis of the same audit: SOUND — no fabricated/misattributed figure.)

- fix(serve): **thin-pack tip-resolvability re-assert on the live ref hot-swap (audit MED, defence-in-depth).** The in-memory hot-swap (`RepoState::apply_cas_push_inmemory`) advanced the advertised tip after a durable CAS push trusting the v0 self-contained-pack invariant; its own comment demanded that "when thin-pack base resolution lands … this call site MUST re-assert tip resolvability before advancing." #206 (thin-pack / CAS-base reachability) landed without it. Closed: `CasRw` now records every existing-closure oid a push resolved a delta base from (`consumed_existing: RefCell<HashSet<String>>`, tracked on the resolution-attempt in `get`; exposed via `consumed_existing_bases()` — sound interior-mut because a `CasRw` is a per-push LOCAL, never shared across threads). `apply_cas_push_inmemory` takes the consumed-base list and, AFTER merging the pushed oids into `live_oid_index` and BEFORE `set_ref`, re-asserts every consumed base is present in `live_oid_index` (a parse error or an absent base → fail-closed `Err`, the ref is NOT advanced — the push stays durable, the prior tip keeps serving, the correct tip loads on reboot, the existing failure path). Today this is safe BY CONSTRUCTION under `max_instances:1` (a consumed base ∈ `CasRw.existing` == R2's `oid-index.json` == the `live_oid_index` boot seed, so the check never fires); the guard catches a FUTURE divergence (HA / a stale live index) ever advertising a tip whose base the read path can't resolve (a clone 500 mid-stream). Tests: refuse-advance when a consumed base is absent from the live index (prior tip still serves), advance when it is present. Plus a LOW honesty guard: `pr land --dispatch` close path asserted to submit `cost_usd_micros: None` (honest-zero until a real provider-/usage source — the per-PR honesty law) via a body-capturing transport. (The `CloseRequest` serde-skip-when-`None` omission is already locked by `wire_conformance::close_request_omits_cost_when_none_and_includes_it_when_some`.)

- test(checks): **mirror the four canonical lease-DTO conformance vectors byte-identical** (anti-drift tripwire, lockstep with corelink-runners #230). Copied `conformance/{AcquireRequest,AcquireResponse,CloseRequest,CloseResponse}.json` verbatim (sha256 in `manifest.sha256`, matched to the Runners TL's published hashes) + `canonical_*_subset_round_trips` tests: deserialize each canonical vector into hugit's client DTO, re-serialize, assert every key hugit produces equals the canonical value. hugit's client DTOs are intentional SUBSETS (request DTOs omit fabric optionals it doesn't send; `CloseResponse` ignores the attestation block), so the tripwire is subset round-trip equality, not blanket byte-identical. All 4 round-trip clean — NO drift; the 3-wire-drift history (acquire-req/-resp/close) is frozen. Added `Deserialize`/`Serialize` to `CloseRequest`/`CloseResponse` (test-only directions, documented).

- fix(deps): **bump `anyhow` `=1.0.102` → `=1.0.103`** to clear RUSTSEC unsoundness in `Error::downcast_mut()` (UB when `.context()` then `downcast_mut`; dtolnay/anyhow#451). Freshly-published advisory was failing `cargo deny` on every PR repo-wide; fixed at root (no ignore-suppression).
- perf(proto): **blob-history carry-forward — ~2x deeper history in the same 2s budget.** The `blob_history` first-parent walk fetched each parent commit TWICE (once to compute `parent_blob`, then again as the next iteration's `current`) and re-resolved the path-blob the next iteration already had. Carry the resolved parent (`CommitInfo` + path-blob oid) forward → one commit-load + one tree-walk per commit instead of two (~2 vs ~4 CAS fetches/commit). Same wall-clock bound, same fail-closed semantics, strictly fewer fetches → the cold-cache walk reaches ~2x deeper before the `BLOB_HISTORY_BUDGET` trips (mitigates deep-history files returning empty). Proven by `carry_forward_halves_the_per_commit_fetches` (fetch-counting `ObjectSource`: < 3*N gets on an N-commit chain).

- feat(proto+contracts+serve): **blob "Histórico" revision-timeline — a per-path git-log VM on the blob read** (githugr NEEDS #3.2). The blob screen's "Histórico" drawer rendered disabled-honest because the engine had no per-file revision history. New `hugit_proto::blob_history(src, head_commit, path, deadline, max_revs) -> Vec<BlobHistoryEntry>` (`crates/hugit-proto/src/read/history.rs`): a first-parent `git log --first-parent <path>` walk over the git-from-CAS commit graph that emits one entry per commit that TOUCHED `path` (its blob oid differs from the first parent's — including add/delete; a root commit containing the path = its introduction). **WALL-CLOCK BOUNDED — the critical constraint:** the walk does N synchronous CAS/R2 fetches on the single-threaded lazy-CAS engine (commit + its tree + path-resolve + parent tree, per commit), so an unbounded deep-history walk would wedge the whole accept loop (the code-search-wedge class). Bounded by BOTH a `BLOB_HISTORY_BUDGET` (2s) wall-clock deadline (mirrors `DIFF_BUDGET`/`CODE_SCAN_BUDGET`, checked once per commit BEFORE any fetch) AND a `MAX_HISTORY_REVS` (50) count cap. **Fail-closed = partial-honest:** a hit bound OR a missing/garbled object mid-walk STOPS the walk and returns the revisions collected so far (newest-first) — NEVER errors (no 500 on the blob read), NEVER fabricates. The VM `BlobHistoryVm { rev_ref, when, author, summary }` is added additively (`#[serde(default)] history: Vec<…>`) to `BlobVm`: render-when-present (empty → the drawer stays disabled-honest; populated → newest-first revisions). `summary` is the commit message FIRST LINE only; `when` humanizes the commit author time. **REDACTION:** `author`/`summary` are free text from commits → SCRUBBED at the read boundary (`build_blob`), a secret-MATRIX test proves a PAT-shaped author/message is `[REDACTED]`. The HEAD commit is the repo's default-branch tip from the live `git_refs` snapshot (reuses the existing `pick_default_branch` picker, now `pub(crate)`; `RepoState::head_commit()`), threaded through `dispatch_repo` → `build_blob(…, head_commit)` → an empty-honest history when `None`. Hermetic + GIT-FREE tests: the walk finds exactly the touching commits on a hand-built 3-commit chain (skips the non-touching one, newest-first), a past-deadline call returns partial without exhausting a 200-commit chain, the count cap bounds the walk, a missing-object mid-walk returns partial not error, deletion is a touch, and the build_blob secret-scrub on author+summary.
- feat(checks+cli): **submit-side of the provider-billed cost on the lease close path (#64).** The CoreLink fabric shipped `CloseRequest.cost_usd_micros: Option<u64>` (#226, additive `#[serde(default)]`, recorded VERBATIM into `CloseResponse.metrics.cost_usd_micros` — provider-billed, never recomputed). hugit's matching submit side: **(1)** added `cost_usd_micros: Option<u64>` to hugit's `CloseRequest` with `#[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]` — when `None` the field is OMITTED so the body stays byte-identical to today's `{"status":"…"}` (accepted by BOTH the not-yet-redeployed fabric with no such field AND the new one), when `Some(n)` it serializes with the EXACT `cost_usd_micros` key the `deny_unknown_fields` fabric expects. **(2)** `LeaseClient::close()` takes a new `cost_usd_micros: Option<u64>` param and sets it on the request body. **(3)** `dispatch_attest_offbox` (the off-box A-path) threads the figure through to the close; the B-path `dispatch_check` passes `None` (a box-exec lease keeps its `poll_meta` figure). **Honesty law:** there is NO real provider-cost source in the dispatch today — the off-box agent loop's `/usage` read is a separate, not-yet-built integration — so `pr land --dispatch` passes `None` (honest-zero floor) with a `TODO(#64)` marking where the real figure will come from; NEVER a derived/misattributed stand-in (the per-PR honesty law). A new `tests/fixtures/wire/CloseRequest_with_cost.json` + a conformance test lock the skip-when-`None` invariant (the `deny_unknown_fields` safety) and the exact `Some(n)` key.
- fix(checks+cli): **`AcquireLeaseRequest` wire-mismatch — the THIRD latent twin of the #204 acquire-wrapper / #205 close bugs, now BLOCKING the live cost-killer.** A real acquire against the live fabric `422`d `unknown field principal_chain`: hugit sent `{principal_chain, path_set, net_policy, ttl_ms}` from an OLD/never-live contract, but the frozen `deny_unknown_fields` fabric DTO (`corelink-fabric-api::dto::AcquireRequest`) accepts ONLY `{image_digest, net_policy, tmp_root, expiry_ms}` — the drift was masked because the lease client is fake-transport-only + PAT-gated, never run live. Fix: **(A)** rewrote `AcquireLeaseRequest` to the four frozen fabric fields ONLY — dropped `principal_chain`/`path_set` (the fabric resolves them server-side from the authenticated tenant + claim, they are NOT sent), renamed `ttl_ms` → `expiry_ms` (the fabric's name for the same u64 TTL-ms); OMITTED `runner`/`toolchain_digest` (the fabric `#[serde(default)]`s them and hugit's classic off-box path never sets them, so a body without them is byte-identical to `None, None`). hugit stays LIBERAL on its own side (no `deny_unknown_fields`); the load-bearing invariant is that it SERIALIZES to exactly the four keys the fabric accepts. **(B)** the `pr land --dispatch` production builder uses `LAND_DISPATCH_IMAGE_DIGEST` — a clearly-labelled all-zero sentinel (`hugit-offbox-attest@sha256:000…0`): hugit's off-box A-mode submits §13 off-box and NEVER execs, so the fabric records this digest but spawns NO box (the fabric only FORMAT-checks `sha256:`, never existence) — an honest "recorded, never spawned" marker, more honest than borrowing a real image's digest; `LAND_DISPATCH_TMP_ROOT = "/work/tmp"`, `expiry_ms` = the existing `LAND_DISPATCH_TTL_MS`, `net_policy` = the existing `deny-all` (the porcelain land-confirm check runs no outbound network). **(C)** a wire-conformance fixture (`tests/fixtures/wire/AcquireRequest.json` = the canonical fabric body) + a roundtrip test pin that hugit's request serializes to EXACTLY `{image_digest, net_policy, tmp_root, expiry_ms}` and carries NONE of the retired fields — so this drift class can't recur (the cross-repo byte-frozen vector is the Runners TL's joint-pass lane, pending).
- feat(contracts+serve): **`CostVm.cost_usd_micros: u64` — unify the money contract** (githugr NEEDS #1). `CostVm.usd` was a pre-divided `f64`; the rest of the contract uses `cost_usd_micros: u64` (ADR-0001, integer micro-USD, no float rounding). Added `cost_usd_micros` additively (serde-default, forward-compat) sourced directly from `rec.cost.total.cost_usd_micros` (the raw u64 the engine already had before the `/1_000_000.0`); `usd` kept DEPRECATED one release (`usd == cost_usd_micros as f64 / 1e6`). Render-when-present: githugr switches the landing PR-drawer cost to `fmt_usd_micros` + drops the inline divide, ZERO further githugr change.
- feat(http-contracts+serve): **structured state enums on 4 view-models — githugr styles from a discriminant, not by substring-matching localized pt-BR prose** (githugr NEEDS #2). ALL ADDITIVE + forward-compat (`#[serde(default)]`); the existing prose `String` fields STAY (githugr keeps substring-matching until it switches), no `deny_unknown_fields`. (1) **`CampaignPrVm.state: PrState`** — REUSES the existing landing-card `PrState` enum (no new enum); populated in `campaign.rs::build_campaign_pr` with the SAME precedence as the `state_label` prose and `landing.rs::pr_state` (landed→`Landed` ≻ abandoned→`Blocked` ≻ `all_pr_queued`→`Queued` ≻ `Open`), making the old `state_label.contains("bloque")||"✗"` inference structural; `PrState` gained `Copy + Default` (`#[default] Open` — the honest absent/forward-compat value). (2) **`VerdictVm.decision: VerdictDecision`** (new `enum {Pass, Warn, Fail}`) — derived via a SINGLE shared `decision_of(outcome: &str)` mapping wired at all 4 build sites (`review.rs`/`attention.rs`/`intent_detail.rs` uppercase `APPROVE`/`FIX-FIRST`/`REJECT` + the `landing.rs`/ledger-fallback `Debug`-of-enum `Approve`/`FixFirst`/`Reject`), case-insensitive + `-`/`_`/space-tolerant: approve/proven→`Pass`, fix-first/changes-requested→`Warn`, reject→`Fail`. CONSERVATIVE: an unrecognized outcome → `Warn` (never silently `Pass`); the `#[default]` variant is `Pass` only for the absent-field forward-compat baseline. (3) **`CampaignWhoVm.role_kind: WhoRole` + `status_kind: WhoStatus`** — `WhoRole` variants MIRROR the engine's real `PrincipalClass::{Human, Orchestrator, Worker, Model}` (no invented roles) + `#[default] Unknown`; `WhoStatus {Unknown, Active, Pending, Done}`. NO live builder constructs `CampaignWhoVm` yet (`build_campaign` emits `who: vec![]`, a STUB) — the fields are carried on the contract additively so githugr lights them the moment a builder lands. (4) **`SearchIntentVm.state: IntentState {Unknown, InFlight, Landed, Rejected}`** — populated in `search.rs::search_intents` from the same source as the `status` prose; today's `intent.landed` projection carries no status, so the honest live value is `Unknown` (mirrors the empty `status`). Every new enum derives `Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize, Default` (matches the sibling `KpiSubKind`/`PrState` pattern). Tests: per-VM round-trip + forward-compat-default tests, and `decision_of` mapped against every canonical outcome (approve→Pass, fix-first→Warn, reject→Fail, unknown→Warn).

- fix(proto): **lower `DEFAULT_MAX_CAS_LOOKUPS` 100_000 → 4_096 (thin-pack CAS-base latency DoS, audit 2026-06-28).** On the single-threaded engine each thin-pack CAS lookup is a synchronous R2 fetch, so the 100k count-bound = ~hours of blocked accept loop (the count-bound-not-latency-bound systemic pattern — same class as the code-search #207 / diff #210 DoS, here on the operator-only write path). A COUNT bound (fail-closed on exceed) is the correct guard for the WRITE path — a push is a transaction, so a wall-clock budget that truncated it mid-resolution would be wrong; reject the pathological push instead. 4_096 covers any honest incremental push by a wide margin (its pushed-vs-CAS frontier is tens-to-low-hundreds of objects) while capping a malicious thin-pack's R2 fan-out to minutes. Audited SOUND alongside: the CAS-base resolution's cross-repo isolation (repo-scoped `<tenant>/<repo>/oid-index.json`).

- fix(proto+serve): **wall-clock budget on `tree_diff` — close a single-threaded-engine DoS in the diff path** (the same class as the 2026-06-27 code-search wedge, found by a proactive read-path audit before a user hit it). `tree_diff` walks two trees fetching each subtree+changed blob via the lazy CAS (a synchronous R2 fetch per object); `MAX_DIFF_FILES=2000` is a RESULT bound, NOT a latency bound, so a cold-cache diff of a large commit blocked the whole accept loop for minutes — and `review`'s PR diffstat ran it PER intent (unbounded loop). Fix: a `DIFF_BUDGET` (2s) wall-clock ceiling checked between every subtree recursion + file row (`tree_diff` = wrapper, `tree_diff_until(deadline)` = testable core, stops with the PARTIAL diff — a diffstat is informational); plus `MAX_REVIEW_DIFF_INTENTS=64` caps how many per-intent diffs one review request runs. Affects `/v1/repos/{repo}/intents/{id}` (intent drawer) + `review` (the review interrogation). Deterministically tested (`tree_diff_wall_clock_budget_stops_before_any_fetch`, a past deadline stops before any fetch — no timing flake). The real fix for useful+safe diffs on a lazy-CAS engine is a pre-built index/cache (tracked with the code-search index).

- fix(serve): **advertise the `delete-refs` capability — unlocks `git push --delete` for the real git client.** The server-side delete handler (#208) was proven live via a raw receive-pack POST, but `git push --delete` via the git CLIENT got "remote rejected" — because the receive-pack advertise omitted `delete-refs`, and per the git protocol a client refuses to send a zero-id (delete) command unless the server advertised it (so git shipped an EMPTY command list, the delete never reached the working handler). Added `delete-refs` to `RECEIVE_CAPS`; regression-tested (`receive_advert_advertises_delete_refs_so_the_client_sends_deletes`). Closes the delete-UX gap so `git push --delete` works end-to-end via the actual client, not just the wire.

- feat(serve): **delete-ref support for receive-pack (`git push --delete <branch>`) — the push wire is now complete (create / update / incremental / DELETE).** v0 REJECTED every delete (`ng delete-not-supported`); it is now a first-class WRITE on the SAME authz as any push (deploy-gate → `two_tier_auth` → `authorize_write` ownership — never weakened, never the read predicate). The serve handler (`handle_delete_ref`) adds, after authz, a pure pre-condition decision (`delete_ref_decision`): **(a) default-branch guard** — REFUSE to delete the repo's default/HEAD branch (the exact `pick_default_branch` pick the advertise resolves HEAD to: `refs/heads/main`, else `master`, else the first head, else the first ref) → `ng refuse-delete-default-branch` (never let a push nuke main); **(b) stale-check against the AUTHORITATIVE live `git_refs` snapshot** (NOT `replay(log)` — the #2a discipline) → `ng delete-of-absent-ref` when the ref is gone, `ng non-fast-forward` when the tip differs from the pusher's `old_oid`; **(c) NO pack / no objects / no reachability** — a delete carries no target, so the proto `receive_pack` unpack path is never entered. Durable removal is fail-closed and `ok` ONLY after it commits: **CAS mode** via the new `cas::finalize_cas_delete` (the `ref.delete` event persisted FIRST via the compare-and-swap log append, THEN `remove_cas_ref_from_manifest` drops `refs[ref_name]` from `refs.json` and PUTs it back — `oid-index.json` UNCHANGED: a git delete-ref drops only the ref pointer, never the objects/no GC, so the deleted tip's closure stays resolvable for any other ref); **GitDir mode** via `git update-ref -d <ref> <old_oid>` (compare-and-swap) after the same log persist. Then `RepoState::apply_cas_delete_inmemory` (`LiveRefs::remove_ref`) drops the ref from this engine's in-memory advertise so the deleted branch stops serving with NO reboot (never ahead of the durable store — the apply runs only after finalize). A manifest/persist fault → a distinct `ng` (`manifest-write-failed`/`log-persist-failed`), never an `ok` and never an in-memory removal without the durable rewrite. **ZERO behavior change** when receive-pack is OFF (flag-gated). Hermetic + GIT-FREE tests (mock R2/CAS, raw receive-pack POST): the decision (default-branch/stale/absent), `finalize_cas_delete` (removes ref, keeps objects + other refs; persist-fault and manifest-PUT-fault both abort fail-closed), the live hot-swap drops the ref from the advertise, plus an end-to-end git-dir flow (real `git push` create → raw delete POST proving `ng refuse-delete-default-branch` on main, `ng non-fast-forward` on a stale tip, `ok` on the valid delete, then both a live-instance and a fresh-instance advertise confirm durable removal while main survives).
- fix(serve): **code-search wall-clock budget — close a single-threaded-engine DoS.** A `/v1/repos/{repo}/search` code query walks the git tree fetching each tree+blob via the lazy CAS (a synchronous R2 fetch per object on a cold cache); the existing `CODE_FILE_CAP` (2000) is a RESULT bound, not a LATENCY bound, so a cold-cache scan of thousands of sequential R2 fetches BLOCKED the single-threaded accept loop for minutes (live wedge 2026-06-27: `/readyz` 000, www blob 503, until the container auto-restarted). Fix: a hard `CODE_SCAN_BUDGET` (2s) wall-clock ceiling checked between every tree pop + blob fetch — the walk stops with partial results (acceptable under the F4c-interim disclaimer; the real fix is a pre-built index) rather than wedging the engine. Deterministically tested via a `Duration::ZERO` budget that stops before any fetch, plus a real-budget sanity that search still finds the hit. No wire-contract (VM) change.

- fix(checks): **`LeaseClient::close` wire-mismatch — the latent twin of the #204 `acquire`-wrapper bug.** `close()` POSTed an EMPTY body and returned `()`, but the real fabric `POST /v1/leases/{id}/close` REQUIRES a `status` body (`"succeeded"`/`"failed"`) AND delivers the finalized §13.1 metrics atomically on `CloseResponse.metrics` — so a LIVE A-mode dispatch would have `400`d at close (masked: the lease client is fake-transport-only + PAT-gated, never run against the live fabric, so the transcription drifted). Fix: **(A)** transcribed `CloseRequest{status}` + `CloseResponse{lease_id, released, capture_incomplete, metrics}` from the frozen fabric DTO (`corelink-fabric-api::dto`), LIBERAL (no `deny_unknown_fields`; the fabric's `attestation`/`result_binding_sig*`/`fabric_key_id`/echoed `check_result` are tolerated-and-ignored), `metrics` REQUIRED (§13.1 — a metrics-less close is a decode error, never a fabricated zero) and mapped to the same `IntentMetrics` via `RunnerJobMetrics`. **(B)** `close(lease_id, CloseStatus)` now POSTs the `status` body and returns `Result<CloseResponse, _>`; always-called + fail-closed (only a `200` + parseable body succeeds; a close error still surfaces, metrics never fabricated). **(C)** the A-mode `dispatch_attest_offbox` ATTESTED figure is now the fabric's finalized `CloseResponse.metrics` (the signed source of truth), NOT the modeled `poll_meta` — the off-box lease carries a §13.2 capture hook so its close yields real finalized metrics; the B-path KEEPS `poll_meta` (a hook-less box lease's close returns the honest-zero projection — preferring it would wipe the box-measured figure); honesty law preserved (honest-zero when the fabric reports zero, full cache-split never flattened). **(D)** hugit-side wire-conformance fixtures (`tests/fixtures/wire/{AcquireResponse_check_lease,AcquireResponse_runner_lease,CloseResponse}.json`) + a roundtrip test pin the shapes hugit must parse so this drift class can't recur (the cross-repo byte-frozen `conformance/{AcquireResponse,CloseResponse}.json` joint pass is the Runners TL's lane, pending). Hermetic (fake transport, zero network); the PAT is the close Bearer (sentinel-asserted), never a scoped credential.
- feat(proto): **thin-pack / CAS-base reachability — an INCREMENTAL `git push` (one that builds on server-side history) now lands.** `receive_pack`'s unpack + reachability were PURE over the pushed set: a REF_DELTA whose base lives server-side (a thin pack) was rejected `MalformedPack`, and a ref whose closure includes an unchanged ancestor already in CAS was rejected `UnreachableTarget` — both block the normal "push a child commit; its parent + unchanged subtrees are already in CAS" case. Two proto changes, both threading the read-only `Cas` already in scope: **(1) CAS-aware reachability** — `target_reachable` now treats an oid absent from the pushed set but present via `cas.contains` as a SATISFIED boundary leaf (stop the walk, like the gitlink skip); only absent-in-BOTH → unreachable. **(2) CAS delta-base resolution** — when the in-pack fixpoint stalls, a remaining REF_DELTA's base is fetched via `cas.get`, decoded, and used as the delta pre-image; the RESOLVED object's oid is still re-derived (`gix_object::compute_hash` — the verify holds); a base in NEITHER pack nor CAS → `MalformedPack` (a true thin base nowhere, no bare oid trusted). The decoder is tolerant of BOTH the trait-documented zlib loose form (`InMemoryCas`/`GitDirCas`) AND the uncompressed framing the live `CasRw` serve adapter returns. **Hardening (fail-closed):** new `RecvLimits.max_cas_lookups` (default 100_000) bounds the new CAS-read surface — each thin-base `get` + each reachability-boundary `contains` counts; exceed → `CasLookupBudgetExceeded`, so a push can never trigger unbounded R2 fan-out (DoS). The aggregate inflated-size cap applies to CAS-base-expanded objects too. **Serve seam already wired:** `CasRw::get`/`contains` resolve a server-side-history git-oid through the persisted `oid-index → blake3 → R2` path (re-read at push time via `open_writer`), so NO serve-side change was needed — only a serve test proving it. All hermetic + GIT-FREE (mock `Cas`; synthetic thin REF_DELTA packs built from raw bytes). v0 caveat closed; clone-back still gated by the private read-gate (public-flag deferred).
- feat(checks+cli): **cost-killer path "A" — the OFF-BOX §13 ingest for real per-PR attested cost.** The CoreLink Runners TL wired the fabric side (PR #202, live on fabricd `93573be4`): the `POST /v1/leases` acquire response now carries an off-box §13.2 ingest credential so hugit's OWN dispatch client (which runs off-box, not in a fabric box) can submit its trajectory directly. hugit side: **(A) transcribed the `AcquireResponse` WRAPPER + fixed a latent wire bug** — `LeaseClient::acquire` parsed the response body FLAT into `RunnerLease`, but the real fabric returns `{lease, exec_endpoint, envelope_ingest?}` (masked because the hermetic fake fed flat JSON and the PAT-gated path never ran live); `acquire` now returns `AcquireResponse` (liberal: only `lease` required, `envelope_ingest` defaults to `None` so a runner-lease response parses byte-identically), and the new `EnvelopeIngest { ingest_path, credential }` redacts the scoped credential in `Debug` (held like the PAT). **(B) `LeaseClient::submit_envelope`** — `POST {base}{ingest_path}` with the SCOPED, write-only ingest credential as the Bearer (NEVER the tenant PAT; two credentials by trust boundary), §13.2 trajectory-event body (`IngestEvent`/`IngestUsage` transcribed from the fabric handler); the credential is per-call, never stored/logged/in-errors, and can only reach the configured host (path concatenated onto base). **(C) `dispatch_attest_offbox`** — the A-mode lifecycle: acquire → submit the off-box trajectory to the §13.2 ingest endpoint (scoped cred) → poll the fabric's signed envelope (PAT) → close (PAT, ALWAYS). FAIL-CLOSED: a check lease whose acquire response has `envelope_ingest = None` errors `NoEnvelopeIngest` — never a silent B-path fall-back or a fabricated envelope; the lease is always closed. `project_intent_metrics` faithfully projects a measured §13.1 `IntentMetrics` aggregate into the §13.2 event batch (token cache-split on the first model_turn, tool_calls + per-tool split as tool_call events). **(D) `hugit pr land --dispatch` now uses the A-mode transport** (submit-to-ingest, not exec+poll); the captured cost is the fabric's signed envelope readback (the runner is the source of truth, never a hand-stamp), and the full §13.1 token cache-split still flows through `EnvelopeMetricsArgs.full_metrics` into capture-on-land. Hermetic (fake transport, zero network); the PAT/scoped-cred discipline is asserted (a sentinel PAT proves the PAT never reaches the ingest endpoint). Live per-PR cost additionally waits on a live off-box measured source + `HUGIT_RUNNER_PAT` provisioning.
- fix(serve+proto): **write-path hardening — 5 findings from the live `git push` (receive-pack) adversarial audit.** (1) **Ref-projection reconciliation [correctness]:** the compare-and-append stale-check derived the current ref view from `replay(log)`, but a CAS-ingested branch has NO `ref.update` event on the log (those come only from application verbs, never git ingest) — so updating an existing ingested branch (the normal case) was FALSE-rejected as `StaleRef`/non-fast-forward (push worked only for NEW branches). Fix: `receive_pack` now takes an authoritative `current_refs` map — the serve path passes its live `git_refs` snapshot (the SAME projection the advertise is built from), the in-memory `SerializedReceiver` passes `replay(log)` (preserving its log-as-truth semantics). (2) **Resolver O(n²) DoS cap:** a pack of reverse-ordered REF_DELTAs resolves ≤1 delta/pass (≈200k entries → ~5e10 ops → freezes the single-threaded engine, stalling reads). New `RecvLimits.max_delta_passes` (default 1024 — generous vs git's ≤50 chain depth) fails closed with `DecompressionBomb`. (3) **Peak unpack memory:** lowered `DEFAULT_MAX_INFLATED_BYTES` 64→32 MiB so the ~3–4× aggregate transient peak (`raws`+`resolved`+`by_oid`+`objects` coexisting) is ~96–128 MiB (container-safe) not ~200–256 MiB; corrected the misleading "peak bounded to max_inflated" comment to state the true aggregate bound. (4) **Cache poison parity:** the lazy-CAS read cache's two `.lock().expect("poisoned")` (the one hot-path lock that ABORTED on poison) now `unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner())`, matching `LiveRefs`/`LiveOidIndex` — a poison no longer bricks object-serving for the repo. (5) **Bounded decoded-object cache:** `LazyCasObjectSource.cache` grew unbounded (every decoded body retained forever → RSS → OOM on a long-lived instance); now a byte-bounded FIFO-eviction `BoundedObjectCache` (default `DEFAULT_OBJECT_CACHE_BYTES` = 64 MiB of bodies; a miss-after-evict re-fetches, content-addressing keeps it correct). All hermetic + GIT-FREE (synthetic reverse-ordered REF_DELTA pack built from raw bytes; FIX-1 invariants over the committed self-contained pack fixture against a seeded prior tip). No behavior change when receive-pack is OFF.
- feat(serve): **live ref hot-swap — a CAS-mode `git push` takes effect IMMEDIATELY, no engine reboot.** The boot-loaded in-memory views were immutable, so the first push to a ref advertised the STALE tip for the rest of the engine lifetime: a SECOND push to that ref was wrongly rejected as a non-fast-forward (the client derives `old` from the stale advertise, while the durably-reloaded log already shows the new tip → false `StaleRef`), and a just-pushed object's oid was not in the boot oid-index, so it could not clone-resolve until reboot. Fix: the per-repo refs map (`RepoState.git_refs` → `LiveRefs`) AND the lazy CAS source's `oid → blake3` index (`LazyCasObjectSource.index` → a shared `LiveOidIndex`) are now **interior-mutable** (`Arc<RwLock<…>>`; the accept loop is single-threaded → uncontended), shared between the git-wire read path (advertise/clone) and the push finalize. On a SUCCESSFUL `finalize_cas_push` (AFTER the durable CAS objects + R2 manifests commit — never before), `RepoState::apply_cas_push_inmemory` merges the pushed objects' `oid → blake3` (reused verbatim from the `CasRw`, never re-read from R2) into the live oid-index FIRST, then advances `ref → new_oid` LAST — the same fail-closed order as the durable manifests, so the advertise never names a tip whose closure isn't already in-memory-resolvable. The in-memory view is never AHEAD of the durable store (a malformed-oid abort leaves the prior tip serving; the push stays durable + reports `ok`, the correct tip loads on the next reboot). **ZERO behavior change** when receive-pack is OFF (flag-gated) or in GitDir mode (its durable `git update-ref` path is untouched — no in-memory hot-swap). Hermetic tests: the advertise reflects the pushed tip + a newly-created ref with no reload; the object source resolves a merged oid with no reboot; a malformed oid fails closed without advancing the ref.
- fix(serve): **panic-isolate the git + SSE handlers** — `respond_git` (clone/fetch + receive-pack) and `respond_sse` ran BEFORE the loop's `catch_unwind` and `continue`d, so a panic in either would unwind the single-threaded accept loop and crash the whole engine (on `max_instances:1`, a full outage / crash-loop). Now each is wrapped in `catch_unwind` (matching `route_with_body`): a panic drops only that one request, the server survives. Defense-in-depth — the receive-pack pack engine is panic-free on attacker bytes by construction, but the live prod write surface must not depend on that audit staying true (adversarial-audit major finding, 2026-06-26).
- feat(cli): **`hugit pr land --dispatch` — real per-PR cost from the runner fabric** (Wave-E PR5). The final wiring of the dispatch wave: `--dispatch` runs the PR's check through `runner::dispatch_check` (acquire → exec → poll → close) and feeds the returned REAL §13.1 `IntentMetrics` into the captured land envelope via `EnvelopeMetricsArgs.full_metrics` — so the land carries the measured per-job cost + the FULL token cache-split (`input/output/cache_read/cache_write/total`), never flattened. **Fail-closed + honest:** an unwired/unreachable runner is a CLEAR error (`runner_not_configured` / `runner_dispatch_failed`) — the PR is NOT settled and NO envelope is captured, never a fabricated or honest-zero stand-in (the user asked for real cost; absence is an error). `--dispatch` is mutually exclusive with the manual `--tokens/…` metric flags (`dispatch_conflict`); the PAT never leaks (asserted). Without `--dispatch`, land is byte-for-byte the existing honest-zero / manual path. Hermetic (fake transport, zero network); live execution additionally waits on the runner fabric's `/v1/leases/{id}/exec` spawn path (the runners TL's lane).
- feat(serve): **git-free pure-Rust pack unpack for receive-pack (distroless git push)** — replaces the `receive_pack` core's `ScratchOdb` (which shelled out to the SYSTEM `git` binary: `unpack-objects`/`cat-file`/`rev-list`) with a pure-Rust `gix-pack` unpacker, so `git push` works on the `gcr.io/distroless/cc` engine (no git binary). `BytesToEntriesIter` (Mode::Verify) + a bounds-checked OFS/REF delta resolver; oid = `gix_object::compute_hash` over the decoded pre-image (the verify — a tampered object computes a different oid); `CasObject.bytes = zlib(<type> <len>\0<body>)` byte-identical to git loose (CasRw/GitDirCas round-trip unchanged); pure-Rust full-closure reachability BFS. Panic-free on attacker-controlled bytes. **Aggregate inflated-size bomb guard** (adversarial-review BLOCKER fix): `finalize` sums every RESOLVED object body (bases + delta-expanded) vs `max_inflated_bytes` and `apply_delta` caps each object at the remaining budget — a `1 base + N deltas` COPY-amplification bomb is rejected fail-closed (peak allocation bounded to `max_inflated`, restoring the parity the old `cat-file %(objectsize)` path had). Deleted ALL git-spawn machinery; `materialize_bare_repo` also git-free. 8 git-free unpacker tests (incl. a real OFS_DELTA round-trip + a present-but-incomplete-closure→unreachable). v0 = self-contained packs only (thin-pack bases + incremental pushes on server-side ancestry rejected fail-closed, a tracked CAS-base-resolution follow-up).
- feat(checks): **runner dispatch orchestration + wired executor** (Wave-E PR3) — `runner::dispatch` (`dispatch_check`/`exec_and_collect`): acquire → exec → poll_meta → close, ALWAYS releasing the lease (even on exec error), returning the frozen `CheckResult` + the §13.1 `IntentMetrics`. `LiveBoxRunnerExecutor` is now generic over the transport, `from_runtime`-configurable, and drives the dispatch when wired (still `BoxNotWired` when unconfigured). Hermetic (fake transport); the PAT never leaks (asserted). The lease-acquire dispatch path now exists end-to-end in code — live only once `HUGIT_RUNNER_PAT` + the runner fabric's exec path are provisioned.
- feat(cli): **land capture carrier preserves the §13.1 token cache-split** (Wave-E PR4) — `EnvelopeMetricsArgs` can now carry a full `IntentMetrics` (`input/output/cache_read/cache_write/total`) so a runner-fed land keeps the memo-economics cache split instead of flattening to `total`. Additive — the manual-flag path stays honest-zero by default.

- feat(serve): **CAS-mode git push finalize** — wires the `CasRw` adapter into receive-pack so a CAS-mode repo (the deployed shape) can accept a push: a `RepoWriter` seam (`open_writer`/`has_write_seam`) branches the handler GitDir-vs-CAS; `finalize_cas_push` runs **objects → log → manifests, each fail-closed** (a flush/persist error aborts BEFORE any manifest advertises a tip whose closure isn't uploaded), and `commit_cas_push_manifests` read-merge-writes `oid-index.json` then `refs.json` (documented single-writer assumption over the unconditional R2 put). Write-authz stays ownership-based — `authorize_write` runs before the writer opens, no read-predicate leak. Still **gated OFF** by default (`HUGIT_SERVE_RECEIVE_PACK`), and live push needs a `cas:rw` write PAT — hermetic only (mock R2/CAS), no default-behavior change. PR2 of the git-push wave.
- feat(checks): **§13.1 runner-metrics → `IntentMetrics` mapping** — `runner::metrics` transcribes the frozen §13.1 table (`RunnerJobMetrics` with the FULL token cache-split `input/output/cache_read/cache_write/total` — never flattened — + `wall_ms/active_ms/tool_calls/tool_breakdown/model_turns/cost_usd_micros`) and maps it into the frozen `IntentMetrics`. Unknown runner fields (e.g. `cpu_ms`) are ignored, not errors; the mapping round-trips `conformance/IntentMetrics.json` (the §13.4 drift tripwire `2d8d2215`) byte-exactly. PR2 of the dispatch wave — feeds real per-job metrics into capture-on-land once the lease client runs live.

- feat(serve): **`repo meta` write verb** — `POST /v1/repos/{repo}/repo/meta` (`RepoMetaReq{visibility, owner_tenant}`) sets a repo's visibility by appending the canonical `repo.meta` authz record the read gate already projects. Operator/owner-only (rides `with_write` → `authorize_write` ownership gate; fail-closed on a non-`public`/`private` value). This is the missing write seam for anonymous public clone — the wire + read-authz already serve public correctly; the 404-for-all was a data gap (no `repo.meta` → fail-safe PRIVATE). NOTE: publishing opens ALL reads (blob/code-search/diff/cost) to anonymous, not just clone — a deliberate per-repo product decision; private stays gated by the unchanged fail-safe default, and public never enables anonymous push (`authorize_write` ignores visibility).
- feat(serve+proto): **`CasRw` buffering write adapter (CAS-mode git push foundation)** — implements the `Cas` trait by INFLATING git's verbatim zlib loose bytes to the read side's `encode_loose` framing and keying by `blake3` (`cas_key`) — the same representation `LazyCasObjectSource::get` serves, so a pushed object is byte-identical to a served one (no corruption). The infallible `put` buffers; an explicit fallible `flush` uploads via `batch_upload`+retry; reads consult the buffer then fall through to the repo closure. Adapter-only (PR1) — not yet wired to `RepoState`/the receive-pack handler (PR2, gated on a `cas:rw` write PAT). Adds `flate2` (`rust_backend`, pure-Rust, already transitively resolved at the same version — no new lock entry).
- feat(checks): **runner lease-acquire client (foundation)** — `runner::lease_client`: a fail-closed HTTP client (`RunnerConfig`+`from_runtime` reading `HUGIT_RUNNER_HOST` + a file-then-env PAT, PAT held private and redacted from Debug/Display/errors) with `acquire`/`exec`/`poll_meta`/`poll_events`/`close` over a `RunnerTransport` (get+post), a real ureq transport + a test fake, and a path-traversal guard on `lease_id`. PR1 of the deferred dispatch wave toward real per-job §13 `IntentMetrics`; hermetic only (not yet wired to `LiveBoxRunnerExecutor` or `capture_on_land`, no live calls).
- feat(checks): **attestation key-set selector** — `attest_keyset::select_attestation_key` selects the published `{keys:[{key_id,pubkey_b64,expires_ms}]}` entry by `key_id` and rejects unknown/expired (`expires_ms <= now_ms`, exclusive at the cutover instant) BEFORE the existing single-key `verify_result_binding_v2`. Transcribed byte-for-field from the runner fabric's selector and pinned by `conformance/attestation_keyset_selection.json` (the 5 accept/reject cases). The selector is SOLO; live enforce is a later gated wave (needs `result_binding_sig_v2`+`key_id` on the AC-HIT envelope).
- test(symbols): broaden the `outline_blob` totality corpus with 11 grammar-shaped classifier-refinement inputs (well-formed + truncated, ×10 languages). Banked the honest result of the prod-panic hunt: enumerating **655 real blobs across both served repos via the un-guarded parse path reproduced ZERO panics and ZERO outline false-negatives** — the #189 panic was grammar-version-specific and is gone under the pinned grammars; the `catch_unwind` guards stay as the correct backstop (a C-grammar abort would not unwind).
- feat(serve): **`/readyz` reports the engine `version`** — `{"ready":true,…,"version":"<deploy-tag>"}` from `HUGIT_SERVE_VERSION` (the deploy tag, set in the container env; `"dev"` when unset, JSON-sanitized). A consumer self-confirms a cutover reached the serving instance without pinging the operator — the version-blind `/readyz` is what masked the 2026-06-25 rollout/decode mismatch. (githugr `engine.Dockerfile` forwards `ENGINE_CACHE_BUST` → `HUGIT_SERVE_VERSION`.)
- perf(symbols): **cache the tree-sitter `Query` per language** instead of recompiling it on every `outline_blob` call. `Query::new` is expensive (worst for the C++ grammar) and was a real `/v1/repos/.../blob` hot-path cost (a `.rs` blob measured ~6s vs ~0.5s for an unparsed file). A per-language `OnceLock` compiles each grammar's query once per process and reuses it; `Query` is `Sync` and valid across instances of the same grammar. Output unchanged (the totality + outline tests cover it).
- fix(serve+contracts): **blob `tree` sidebar now emits the repo-relative `path`** — the TRUE cause of the live ver-código 503 (the prior outline-panic guard was defense-in-depth but not the trigger). `BlobTreeRowVm` was missing the `path` field the window deserializes as REQUIRED to build `/r/{repo}/blob/{path}` links; the engine omitted it → the window's serde decode hard-failed → `ENGINE_UNAVAILABLE` (503). This was masked while `blob` 503'd anyway, and surfaced once `blob` returned 200. Fix: add `path` to `BlobTreeRowVm` (serde-default for back-compat) and populate it in `build_tree_sidebar` (parent-dir + name, trailing slash for directories, scrubbed at the read boundary). `edit` was unaffected (no tree sidebar) — the blob/edit asymmetry. + a regression test covering root/nested/dir-slash.
- fix(serve): **`/v1/repos/{repo}/blob/{path}` 503 regression** (ver-código file-viewer DOWN in prod). A tree-sitter panic inside `compute_outline` → `hugit_symbols::outline_blob` was degraded by the per-request `catch_unwind` to `503 ENGINE_UNAVAILABLE` for the WHOLE file read (while `edit/{path}` served the identical CAS bytes at 200). Fix: the blob handler fail-closes the outline to honest-empty on panic, and `outline_blob` now honors its own "never panics" totality contract (+ a totality fuzz test over every language). The exact prod-panicking input + the real parser root-fix + caching the per-call `Query::new` recompile are tracked follow-ups; the guards make the read total regardless.
- fix(serve): checks fleet **`cache_hit_rate_pct` is now REAL-from-rows** (reuses the KPI hit-rate already computed from `check.recorded` events) instead of a hardcoded zero. `cache_saved_usd`/`cache_saved_runner_h` stay honest-empty pending the runner-fabric per-check cost seam (fabricating would repeat the reverted #113 demo-cost).
- feat(serve): **`spend_proof` threaded into the cost-xray + ledger projection** — the `cas:` content-address of the landed envelope (via `hugit_ledger::envelope::cold_ref_for` over the raw stored payload bytes; byte-exact, no serde drift). Stays `None` in prod until real per-job metrics land; lights up githugr's render-when-`Some` attestation marker. Additive + `serde(default)` (backward-compatible).
- feat(serve): **git `push` (receive-pack) — BUILT**. Wires the smart-HTTP `git-receive-pack` advertisement + POST into `hugit_proto::receive_pack` (bound→unpack→verify→store→anchor→append) + `report-status`. Gated fail-closed: OFF unless `HUGIT_SERVE_RECEIVE_PACK=1` AND the repo has an on-disk write seam AND the pusher passes `authorize_write` (ownership, 404-no-oracle). A real `git push` succeeds end-to-end (hermetic test) + clones back. v0: single non-delete ref, self-contained pack (incremental/thin-pack CAS-aware reachability + same-process ref hot-swap are follow-ups).
- feat(serve): receive-pack `report-status` response builder (`receive_wire::build_report_status` + `RefOutcome`) — emits the v1 `unpack ok|<err>` + per-ref `ok`/`ng <reason>` pkt-lines a `git push` client reads. WP2 building block; handler still 403.
- feat(proto): `GitDirCas` — a git-dir-backed `Cas` write adapter for the receive-pack path (writes verbatim loose objects to `objects/<xx>/<rest>`, the write-side analogue of the git-dir read source). WP2 building block of git `push`; not yet wired to the serve handler (push still 403).
- feat(serve): receive-pack wire parser (`receive_wire::parse_receive_pack_body`) — WP1 of git `push`. Pure, fail-closed pkt-line command decoder (`<old> <new> <ref>` + caps + packfile split); no handler behaviour change yet (push still 403). Design: `docs/plan/2026-06-22-receive-pack-wave-design.md`.
- fix(deps): bump `memmap2` 0.9.10 → 0.9.11 (RUSTSEC-2026-0186 — unsound unchecked pointer offset; transitive via gix-pack). Root fix, no audit ignore.
- feat(cas): harden the git-ingest / CAS path — stream one `git cat-file --batch` (was ~2 git spawns/object), back off+retry on `429`/`503`, and split a batch-upload on a transport timeout (idempotent). Makes ingesting a repo's full closure converge under load, and hardens the live engine's git-from-CAS reads against rate limits.
- feat(serve): **multi-repo engine** — `AppState` serves many repos (a `repo → RepoState` map); `/v1/repos/{repo}/…` + the git wire resolve the requested slug per-request, unknown repo → uniform 404 (no oracle). `HUGIT_SERVE_CAS_REPO`/`HUGIT_SERVE_GIT_DIR` now accept a comma-separated set (one member = the old single-repo behavior, unchanged). The foundation for serving a 2nd live repo (F6a) — one forge, many repos.
- feat(cli): **live CoreLink AC wired into `check run` + `land queue`** — memoized checks now hit the hot, shared, content-addressed CoreLink action-cache when the runtime config is present (`HUGIT_CORELINK_AC_URL` + `HUGIT_CORELINK_TENANT` + the PAT file), falling back to the file-backed local AC otherwise (no silent network on an unconfigured box; `--ac` forces local). Smoke-verified live against the provisioned P2 tenant: MISS→remote-HIT, no local `.ac` — the memoization economic core is live.
- feat(wedge): **`hugit land queue` — the union-test + bisect + memoize engine is now invocable.** Batch-land the queued PRs of a campaign: runs `evaluate_union` over their union with memoized, AC-backed checks (re-run = cache HIT, zero re-execution), and on a red union runs `bisect_failure` to isolate the minimal failing pair — landing the green remainder, excluding the culprit, recording a `queue.union_fail` (so `queue show`'s `conflict_pair` lights up). `land` graduated reserved→real. Real for a single-tenant LOCAL operator today (file-backed AC); the distributed runner fabric swaps in behind the same `MemoCheck` trait.

- feat(legibility): **Phase-2 — the killer-data levers** (WP-F2 + review legibility).
  - **WP-F2 capture-on-land:** landing an intent/PR now captures the ADR-0001 context envelope (cost/tokens/model/refs) onto the canonical log via optional orchestrator-metrics flags — the cost/insights surfaces stop reading honest-zero the moment metrics are passed (real on the dogfood path; honest-zero otherwise, never faked).
  - **Review legibility (serve):** real git tree-diff (file + hunk counts, `HUGIT_SERVE_GIT_DIR`-gated) in the review/intent surfaces; `VerdictVm.reviewer` = the verdict's model; transcript blob-fetch from the CAS refs; the attention feed triage-sorted by blast radius; the review evidence store broadened to `journal.note` + envelope charter. Honest-default where no git/CAS source.

- feat(product): **Wave-1 product refinement** (the "fit like a glove" pass).
  - **CLI git-proximity:** `--log` now defaults to `$HUGIT_LOG` else `.hugit/log.json` (no more passing it every time); a fresh repo auto-inits (absent default log = empty, no ceremony); `check run|show|key` (merged `check`+`checks`); `verdict approve|reject` (folded the top-level `approve`/`reject`); `pr queue` + `pr land` (replaced `pr land --settle`); top-level `note` (was `journal note`); bare `hugit` prints help on a TTY; internal jargon stripped from `--help`.
  - **serve:** `git push` returns a clear 403 message (not a silent 404); `/readyz` exposes `git_serving`; interim real code search over the git tree; `GET /v1/orgs/{name}` (thin real `OrgVm`).
  - **contracts (additive, non-breaking):** raw-integer cost fields (`cost_*_micros`/`*_count`) + `spend_proof` alongside the display strings; F5 wedge fields — per-PR `queue_position`/`eta_seconds` on the PR card, `conflict_pair`, `cache_efficiency_pct` (real where data exists, honest-null otherwise).
  - **docs:** README/product lead with the value couplet + an honest live/next/roadmap status table; surfaced `export`/`import`/`undo`/`symbol`; one beachhead ICP.

- chore(redact): break the contiguous secret signatures in the scrubber's FAKE test-vectors (Slack/SendGrid/Stripe) via runtime-invariant `\x` escapes — identical bytes at runtime (redaction + tests unchanged), but the source no longer trips GitHub push-protection / secret-scanning, so the public mirror keeps push-protection ON.

- fix(security): **HTTP/git-wire DoS + SSRF hardening** (convergence-audit wave 2) — cap `list_tree_at_dir` (2000 entries) + path depth (64); cap `symbol --ref` blob read (10 MiB) + reject leading-dash refspec; SSRF-allowlist `HUGIT_SESSION_EXCHANGE_URL` (trusted host suffixes, fail-closed); cap the CAS error-body read (64 KiB) and `search` `q` (1024 B). (upload-pack pack-size accounting deferred — needs protocol flow-control.)

- fix(security): **convergence-audit fixes before open-source.** A 10-agent re-audit closed two go-public showstoppers + redaction leaks:
  - **fix(serve): admin/audit control-plane is operator-only** (not repo-visibility) — making the repo public no longer exposes `/v1/.../audit|erasure|admin` to anonymous callers (404, no oracle).
  - **ci: fork-guard the self-hosted gate** — forked PRs no longer run on our self-hosted runner (`head.repo.fork == false`).
  - **fix(redact): scrub avatar/branch/tree/ref names + case-fold `Bearer` + add `glpat-`** (secrets could ride out via those fields).
  - **chore: public-set cleanup** — personal name/tenant/host placeholders in test fixtures, `.gitleaks.toml` allowlist, SECURITY.md reporting channel, README license badge + quick-start, generic client-facing R2 error.

- chore: **comprehensive infra-recon sanitize** (pre-open-source) — runner IP/hostname, Clerk dev host, GitHub App id, and tenant UUIDs scrubbed from source/tests/scripts + the engine-snapshot seed (placeholders; tests green).

- feat(cli): **make the wedge visible — `hugit checks` + `hugit queue show`.** The product thesis (union
  landing queue + memoized CI) was built but unreachable from the porcelain (SOTA-audit P1). `hugit checks`
  now surfaces the 3-axis memo key (`tree ‖ def ‖ toolchain`), HIT/MISS, and the cached proof ref;
  `hugit queue show` surfaces the union-batch composition, state machine, and the implicated PR on a
  UNION-FAIL. Projects only real recorded fields — honest `null`/`recorded:false` where the data isn't
  event-sourced yet (cached proof-refs + the bisected failing-pair, both tracked to wire next).

- feat(serve): **`blob.tree` file-tree sidebar** — `GET /v1/repos/{repo}/blob/{*path}` now lists the
  current directory's entries (via a new fail-closed `hugit_proto::list_tree_at_dir`, symlinks excluded)
  instead of the honest `[]` default.

- feat(cli): **`hugit symbol --ref <ref>`** — outline a file from a committed git ref (not just a local
  working-tree file), resolving the path through `resolve_blob_at_path` over a lazy `git cat-file` source.

- chore: scrub the personal email `gustavo@humangr.com` from ~13 test fixtures (→ `owner@example.com`) ahead of open-source.

- chore: **pre-open-source hardening (Apache-2.0) + adversarial-audit fixes.** An 11-agent adversarial
  sweep ahead of going open-source (`docs/review/2026-06-20-adversarial-pre-opensource-audit.md`)
  surfaced fixes, all landed together:
  - **fix(serve): D14 authz enforced on the live write boundary** — write verbs derived the principal
    class from a hardcoded literal (matrix was decorative); now derived from the authenticated principal
    chain, fail-closed. `authorize_read` split `Anonymous` (may read Public — the git-clone gate) from
    `Unknown` (a present-but-unclassifiable bearer → denied everywhere).
  - **fix(ledger,policy): close secret-redaction misses** — DigitalOcean `dop_v1_` tokens, bare 32-char
    hex, more keyword prefixes (`auth_token`/`access_token`/`private_key`/…), `Bearer`+any-whitespace;
    the policy pre-commit secrets gate now routes content through the full `redact::apply` (catches
    high-entropy unprefixed secrets that the structural-only gate missed).
  - **fix(serve,proto): robustness/DoS** — reject symlink (mode 120000) blobs (fs-path leak + scrub
    bypass); 10 MiB blob cap; bounded search scan; 256-byte Idempotency-Key cap; poisoned-mutex `.expect()`
    on webhook + AC request paths replaced with `into_inner()` recovery (no crash).
  - **chore: Apache-2.0** LICENSE + per-crate license fields; sanitized infra recon-data from source
    (runner IP, secret paths, personal email); hardened `.gitignore`; added SECURITY/CONTRIBUTING/CoC;
    honesty true-up of README + CLAUDE.md.

- feat(serve): **lazy / on-demand CAS object load — boot reads manifests only, not the whole closure.**
  The git-from-CAS source (`HUGIT_SERVE_CAS_URL` → blob/edit/outline + clone/fetch) used to **eagerly**
  load the entire object closure into memory at boot — one CAS read per object. For the launch repo
  (6,862 objects) that meant ~200s of sequential per-object reads, blowing past the Cloudflare Container
  start window (the container is killed) and re-paid on every scale-to-zero cold start (the 2026-06-20
  engine outage). New `LazyCasObjectSource`: boot reads ONLY `refs.json` + `oid-index.json` (a few KB)
  and resolves HEAD's tree (2 on-demand fetches); each object is fetched from the CAS the first time it
  is served, blake3 + git-SHA-1 double-verified (fail-closed via the new `PackError::Source`), and cached.
  `git_source` becomes `Arc<dyn ObjectSource>`; the distroless / no-git-binary invariant is preserved.
  Also: CAS batch read/exists now chunk at `BATCH_REQUEST_CHUNK` (256), not the frozen 2,000 cap, with a
  90s client timeout — a 2,000-object batch makes the CAS do ~2,000 R2 round-trips in one request and
  times out (this also unblocked the `git-ingest` dedup probe). Verified: local boot against the LIVE CAS
  binds in **8s** (was >200s); 281 existing + 2 new hermetic lazy-source tests green.

- fix(serve): **`load_git_dir` boots in seconds, not minutes — one `git cat-file --batch`, not 2 spawns/object.**
  The git content-seam loader (`HUGIT_SERVE_GIT_DIR` → blob/edit/outline reads + clone/fetch) spawned
  TWO `git` subprocesses per object (`cat-file -t` + `cat-file <type>`) over `rev-list --objects --all`.
  The launch repo has 6,854 objects → ~13.7k spawns → boot took MINUTES, past the container healthcheck
  window — and boot is fail-closed, so a slow boot would mark the container unhealthy = a broken/rolled-
  back deploy (this blocked the STEP-2 git-serving deploy). Now the reachable oid set is streamed through
  ONE long-lived `git cat-file --batch` process (oids on a writer thread, stdout drained concurrently to
  avoid a pipe-buffer deadlock, stderr on a third thread), parsing the `<oid> <type> <size>\n<body>\n`
  framing — byte-identical bodies, same `CasObjectSource` re-hash invariant. Verified on the real repo:
  boot 9s (was minutes), `git clone` of all 6,854 objects succeeds, cloned HEAD == served HEAD, `git
  fsck` clean. Unblocks STEP 2.

- feat(symbols): **multi-language symbol outline — Rust-only → 8 more languages (SOTA).**
  `hugit-symbols` now outlines TypeScript (+TSX), JavaScript, Python, Go, Java, C, C++ and Ruby in
  addition to Rust — closing the "only speaks Rust" prototype gap. One shared `outline_with` engine
  drives every language; each language pairs a tree-sitter `.scm` query with a per-language
  `Classifier` closure that handles what a query alone can't express (C++ callable disambiguation,
  Python/Ruby method-vs-function by enclosing scope, Go `type:`-child struct/interface/type, JS
  arrow-vs-variable). `SymbolKind` gains the SOTA vocabulary (function/method/constructor/class/
  interface/namespace/field/variable/constant/module) alongside the unchanged Rust kinds; the frozen
  `OutlineItemVm` wire shape is untouched (kind is a free string — githugr adds icons for the new
  kinds, non-blocking). The contract holds for every language: deterministic, total (non-UTF-8/binary/
  oversized >2 MiB/unparseable → empty, never panic), 1-based lines, source-ordered. Each grammar is
  `=`-pinned (X4) and `cargo deny`-clean (the existing tree-sitter skip-tree covers the build-dep dups).
  67 hermetic tests across the languages. Built in parallel (8 worktree-isolated agents, one per
  language) then consolidated + cold-verified centrally.

- feat(cli): **`hugit review` — grounded-evidence Q&A over the log (D7), graduated from reserved.**
  `hugit review --log <path> --question <text> [--intent <id>]` answers a human-review question
  STRICTLY by grounded retrieval over evidence projected from the log's `check.recorded` /
  `verdict.recorded` records, reusing the engine's own `verdict::qa::answer_question` +
  `EvidenceStore`. Returns either `{"answer":"cited","citations":[…],"excerpts":[…]}` (every citation
  a real evidence ref) or `{"answer":"refused","reason":…}` — it NEVER fabricates. Honest-thin by
  design: the forever-log scrubs rich bodies on append, so evidence is the surviving structured
  metadata (check name/exit/cache, verdict aggregate/lenses); a question needing a full stdout/diff
  body legitimately refuses until the served-evidence (CAS) path is live. Empty `--question` →
  `empty_question` (exit 2); missing log → `log_not_found`. `--intent` scopes verdict evidence
  (matched scrubbed). Does not shadow git (X5); no-drift oracle holds. With `ctx` (#166), this
  completes PR-D.

- feat(cli): **`hugit ctx resume` — short-horizon session resume (D11), graduated from reserved.**
  Reconstructs a crashed/replaced agent's session from the canonical log's `journal.note` records,
  WITHIN the supported 7-day horizon, reusing the engine's own
  `hugit_ledger::journal::ctx_resume_from_journal` + horizon enforcement (local ≡ any future serve
  mirror). Emits a stable JSON `{workspace_id, intent_id, tenant_id, entries, last_recorded_at,
  horizon_ms, reconstructed:true}`. **Honest refusal, never a silent stale reconstruction:** beyond the
  horizon → `beyond_horizon` (with `age_ms`/`horizon_ms`), an empty binding → `empty_journal`, missing
  log → `log_not_found` — all exit 2. The binding join scrubs `--workspace`/`--intent` the SAME way
  the writer scrubbed them (else a secret-shaped binding would silently miss). `--now-ms` overrides the
  clock; since `journal.note` records are written with `recorded_at=0` (clock-untrusted forever-log),
  resuming a LOCAL log needs `--now-ms` in that time base (documented). `ctx snap` stays P2-gated. Does
  not shadow git (X5); no-drift oracle holds.

- feat(cli): **`hugit symbol --file <path>` — the W6 semantic index gets a CLI surface.**
  Graduated from reserved to a REAL verb: reads a local source file, derives its language from the
  extension (`hugit_symbols::lang_for_ext`, the single source of truth), and emits the symbol outline
  as stable JSON (`{file, lang, outline:[{kind,name,line}]}`) under the WB0 one-error/one-exit law.
  Reuses the SAME `hugit_symbols::outline_blob` producer the serve `/v1` blob outline uses (one source
  of truth — CLI and forge agree by construction). An unsupported/extensionless file is an honest empty
  outline (`lang:null`), never an error; a missing file is `{"error":{kind:"file_not_found",…}}`, exit
  2. Symbol names are scrubbed at the read boundary (no redaction bypass). `symbol` does not shadow a
  git command (X5-verified); the no-drift oracle holds (registry == dispatched).

- feat(serve): **the W6 symbol outline is now LIVE on `GET /v1/repos/{repo}/blob/{*path}`.**
  `build_blob` fills the previously honest-default `outline: []` with a real outline computed from the
  resolved blob bytes via `hugit_symbols::outline_blob` (extension → `lang_for_ext`, the single source
  of truth; unsupported/extensionless files → empty, never fabricated). Symbol `name`s pass through the
  same `scrub` read-boundary as the line text, so a secret-shaped identifier (e.g. a `const`/`static`
  name) is redacted in the outline too — the outline can't become a redaction bypass past the
  line-level scrub. `kind` is the frozen wire vocabulary; the consumer VM (`OutlineItemVm`) is
  unchanged. New tests cover multi-kind capture, unsupported-lang-empty, and the symbol-name scrub.

- test(perf): **de-flake the N-2 reconcile scaling test (ratio headroom + larger base).**
  `reconcile_in_sync_scales_linearly_not_quadratically` flaked on the contended runner at 4.19×
  against a `< 4×` bound (a docs-only PR, so pure machine noise). The ratio assertion was right but
  the band sat too close to the linear/quadratic midpoint and the 2k base was noise-dominated (fixed
  per-run overhead inflates a small baseline's ratio). Fixed by raising the probes to 3k→7.5k (same
  2.5× event ratio, but the larger base amortizes fixed overhead → ratio now ~2.3–2.6× locally) and
  widening the band to `< 5×` — still a clear gap below the quadratic 6.25× signal. Stays a RATIO
  assertion (never absolute wall-clock); the O(n²) regression guard is intact.

- docs: **honesty correction — `policy edit` is REAL (not deferred), and the W6 status is updated.**
  The `policy/mod.rs` module doc + CLAUDE.md still described `hugit policy edit` as deferred ("no
  gate-set mutation/persistence model yet"), but it is fully wired: it folds prior `policy.change`
  events over the `house_gates` baseline and appends a Human-only `policy.change` (the append-only log
  IS the gate-set store). CLAUDE.md's "absent entirely" bullet is corrected: the `hugit-symbols` crate
  is now built (consumers not yet wired); `fleet`/`ledger`/`watch`/`diag` are graduated. The code, not
  the stale narrative, is ground truth.

- feat(symbols): **new `hugit-symbols` crate — the W6 semantic-index / symbol-outline producer (marquee gap).**
  The first slice of the whitepaper "semantic index": `outline_blob(Lang, &[u8]) -> Vec<SymbolItem>`
  parses a blob with tree-sitter and returns a deterministic, flat outline of top-level AND nested
  declarations (Rust first: fn/struct/enum/trait/impl/mod/const/static/macro/type). Pure + headless
  (no I/O, no serde); `SymbolKind::as_wire_str` emits the FROZEN wire vocabulary that maps 1:1 onto the
  existing `hugit_http_contracts::blob::OutlineItemVm`. Total + bounded: non-UTF-8/binary/oversized
  (>2 MiB)/unparseable input returns an empty outline, never a panic/OOM. The grander AC-memoized
  cross-tenant index wraps this pure core later (as the check executor wraps check execution). Deps
  `tree-sitter`/`tree-sitter-rust`/`streaming-iterator` are exact-pinned (X4); `cc` was already a
  workspace build-dep, so this adds vendored C *grammar* source, not a new toolchain class; a justified
  `deny.toml` skip-tree covers the build-dep-only indexmap→hashbrown dup. Consumers (serve blob outline,
  `hugit symbol` verb) wire in a follow-up. 12 hermetic tests; `cargo deny` green.

- fix(checks): **the orphan reaper can no longer SIGKILL an unrelated process (PID-reuse TOCTOU).**
  The descendant sweep added for orphan reaping enumerated PIDs, then killed the direct child, then
  signalled the enumerated PIDs — but between enumeration and signalling a grandchild can exit and its
  PID be RECYCLED by an unrelated process, so `kill -KILL` would hit a stranger, violating the
  reaper's own load-bearing invariant ("never signal beyond our own subtree"). Fixed by (a) reaping
  descendants BEFORE the direct child (while the subtree linkage is still live) and (b) re-verifying,
  immediately before signalling, that each PID's `/proc` ancestry STILL terminates at the direct child
  (`is_descendant_of` — a fresh upward PPid walk); a recycled PID now parented elsewhere is skipped.
  The window shrinks to check-then-immediately-kill, with OS-reaps-on-exit as the backstop. (Audit
  finding on the #159 reaper.)
- fix(ledger): **the fleet projection no longer drops distinct agents/workspaces with secret-shaped ids.**
  `FleetState::from_records` redacted `agent_id`/`workspace_id` BEFORE using them as `HashMap` keys, so
  two distinct secret-shaped ids both collapsed to the single `[REDACTED]` key and collided — the
  second `agent.assigned` overwrote the first and a `completed`/`failed` updated the wrong entry
  (silent state-corruption / agent loss, not a leak). Now the fold keys on the RAW id and redacts ONLY
  at the final emission (the same raw-index pattern the `Ledger` fold uses), so distinct entities stay
  distinct while no raw id is ever surfaced. New acceptance test plants two distinct secret-shaped
  agents + asserts both survive AND that no raw secret appears at the verb boundary.
- fix(ledger): **`hugit watch` routes the rendered line's `kind` through redaction too (defense-in-depth).**
  `render_record` redacted only the payload, embedding `kind` verbatim; now every record-derived string
  in the line goes through the redaction filter (each component separately, so a secret in one field
  can't collapse the whole structured line). Numeric `seq`/`recorded_at` cannot carry a secret.
- fix(ledger): **`hugit fleet` now actually calls `FleetState::validate()` before emitting.**
  The schema doc claimed "validated on every emission" but the verb never called it; the verb now
  validates (a failure is an internal fault, exit 1) so the doc claim is true rather than aspirational.

- fix(cli): **`hugit check` timeouts reap a backgrounded grandchild again — without an unsafe process-group signal.**
  When a checked command backgrounds a grandchild (`foo & …`), the linux `sh -c` FORKS it, so killing
  the direct child by PID left the orphan running past the deadline (the OS only reaped it on hugit's
  exit). The prior fix had REMOVED the process-group (negative-pid) kill that used to reap it, because
  a group signal took down the whole CI job on a GitHub-hosted linux runner even under `setsid` (and
  the prod engine is linux too). This restores orphan reaping WITHOUT a group signal: on linux the
  timeout path enumerates the child's transitive descendants from `/proc` (PID→PPid map, robust
  `stat` parse) BEFORE killing the child — once it dies its children reparent to init and the linkage
  is lost — then SIGKILLs each by its single POSITIVE PID (`kill -KILL <pid>…`), so a process-group
  signal is structurally impossible. macOS `sh -c` execs the command (no forked grandchild) and has no
  `/proc`, so the sweep is linux-only; the prompt-timeout guarantee is unchanged on every host. Closes
  the tracked follow-up to the process-group-kill removal.

- feat(cli): **`hugit fleet` + `hugit watch` go REAL — the rest of the Phase-D forge read surface.**
  Both graduate from `HUGIT_RESERVED_VERBS` to `HUGIT_VERBS` with REAL wiring (no stubs), reusing the
  engine's own projections so every surface agrees by construction. `hugit fleet --log <path>` emits
  the versioned `hugit_ledger::FleetState` schema (workspaces + agents, `last_seq`, `event_count`,
  `malformed`) projected from `ws.state.*` / `agent.*` events — identifiers redacted, malformed
  payloads counted not fabricated. `hugit watch --log <path> [--class <c>]` replays the classified,
  redacted event stream via `hugit_ledger::WatchDisplay` (`landing` / `verdict` / `policy-change` /
  `ws-state` / `other`); the live SSE tail stays the serve surface, render latency is deliberately
  not surfaced (a static replay must be reproducible). Both obey the WB0 one-error/one-exit law; the
  no-drift oracle holds (dispatched == registry); neither shadows a git command (WP-X5).

- feat(cli): **`hugit ledger` goes REAL — the default forge history view (Phase D, graduated from RESERVED).**
  `hugit ledger --log <path> [--campaign <name>]` projects the canonical event log into the
  asked→done→proven history, reusing the SAME `hugit_ledger::Ledger` per-intent fold that
  `campaign show` / `queue show` read — so the ledger view AGREES with every other surface by
  construction (one projection, never a second source of truth). Emits stable JSON: a per-campaign
  rollup (`asked` / `done` / `proven` / `rejected`) + the redacted ledger entries; `--campaign`
  scopes the view. Honest-null on a log with no `intent.landed` (entries `[]` + a disclosing `note`),
  and the WB0 one-error/one-exit law (missing log ⇒ exit 2, `{"error":…}`). `--live` (the SSE tail)
  stays the serve surface. Verb graduated `HUGIT_RESERVED_VERBS` → `HUGIT_VERBS`; the no-drift oracle
  (`acceptance_rcli` ⑥) holds (dispatched == registry).
- fix(dogfood): **the dogfood envelope's spawn lifespan can no longer invert (`died_at < born_at`) — a flaky CI failure.**
  The intent/session envelopes read the wall clock a SECOND time for `Spawn.born_at` *after* the
  `TrajectoryRecorder` already captured its own authoritative `born_at` at `start()`. `finish()`
  computes `died_at = recorder.born_at + elapsed`, so for a sub-millisecond run (the AC-hit hermetic
  path) `elapsed.as_millis() == 0` ⇒ `died_at == recorder.born_at`; if the second clock read had
  crossed a millisecond tick, the envelope's `born_at` landed one ms LATER than `died_at`, inverting
  the lifespan and tripping `acceptance_f2_dogfood`'s `died_at >= born_at` assertion. Now both come
  from the recorder's single anchor (`t.born_at`); the dead second-read helper `unix_ms_now` is
  removed. Latent on every host; surfaced intermittently on the ubuntu CI runner.
- **BREAKING (cli): the `hugit repo meta set` verb is renamed to `hugit meta set`.**
  git 2.54 added a real `git repo` builtin, and the WP-X5 namespace law forbids any hugit verb
  shadowing a git command — so the verb yields the name to git (the "embrace, don't assault /
  don't deviate from git" doctrine). The redundant middle layer is dropped (`repo meta set` →
  `meta set`). The **on-the-wire event kind is unchanged** (`repo.meta`, a frozen authz contract
  the engine consumes): only the CLI surface renamed. Surfaced by the self-hosted-Mac → ubuntu CI
  migration: the dev Mac runs git 2.51 (no `git repo`) so it passed there; the ubuntu runner runs
  git 2.54 and correctly flagged the collision.
- fix(invariants): **the WP-X5① no-shadow oracle now sources git's OWN commands, not `git help -a`.**
  `git help -a` enumerates ambient external `git-*` binaries found on `PATH` (a CI runner's
  third-party `git-repo` tool), making the namespace check host-dependent. It now shells
  `git --list-cmds=builtins,main` (git's compiled-in + porcelain commands — deterministic across
  machines) and parses one-command-per-line. The dead `parse_git_verbs` parser is removed in favor
  of `parse_git_cmd_list`.
- fix(cli): **`hugit check` timeout no longer process-group-signals — it can't take down its own caller (prod + CI safety).**
  The timeout path `kill -<pid>` the check's process GROUP (negative pid) to also reap backgrounded grandchildren.
  But a process-group signal took down the **whole CI job** on a GitHub-hosted linux runner — even with the child
  in its own `setsid` session (POSIX says that should isolate it; the hosted runner's process model cancels the
  job on any negative-pid signal in its tree regardless). Same hazard in prod: the engine container is **linux**,
  so a timed-out check could SIGTERM the engine's process tree. The self-hosted Mac masked it (macOS EPERM'd the
  group-kill); the migration off the Mac exposed it. **Fix:** `kill_group` now SIGKILLs **only the direct child's
  PID** (the `sh -c` execs the command, so its PID is the real process — the timeout is fully satisfied), and the
  output-drain join is **skipped on the timeout path** so a shell-forked grandchild holding the pipe open can't
  delay the return (linux `sh -c` forks `sleep`; macOS execs it). Narrow cost: a check that *backgrounds* a
  grandchild can leave it past the deadline — the OS reaps it on hugit's exit. The corresponding acceptance test
  now asserts the **prompt timeout** (the load-bearing guarantee) rather than orphan-reaping. (Re-introducing
